:
The mass dependence of
\bar{\mathcal{R}}^{(q;Q,\bar{Q})}_{(3;T,2)}
defined in Eq. (
121
).

Interactions with light-flavored Glauber (anti)quarks in the nuclear medium admit a new channel for heavy-quark production depicted in Fig.
7
(b), which is not available to jets showering in the vacuum, such as those created in proton-(anti)proton collision. Any quark-induced enhancement of heavy flavor in relativistic heavy-ion collisions is sensitive to the quark content within the nuclear medium. Of course, there is another source of heavy quark production, one where the gluon with momentum
\ell
is produced by a gluon-gluon fusion, with momenta
p_{1}
and
k
, respectively, in a process similar to Fig.
21
(b). In the case of the QGP, the process of Fig.
7
(b) and Fig.
21
(b), is only allowed following the initial glasma dynamics, i.e. once light quarks have started to hydrodynamize heading towards the QGP. Defining a dimensionless quantity
\bar{\mathcal{R}}^{(q;Q,\bar{Q})}_{(3;T,2)}
for a heavy-quark production channel in kernel-3 [Fig.
7
(b)] as

\bar{\mathcal{R}}^{(q;Q,\bar{Q})}_{(3;T,2)}=\frac{q^{-}\boldsymbol{\ell}^{4}_{2\perp}\mathcal{R}^{(q;Q,\bar{Q})}_{(3;T,2)}}{\left[\frac{C_{F}C_{A}}{2}\right]},

(121)

allows to investigate medium-induced heavy-flavor production, including top quarks, as depicted in Fig.
11
.
14
14

14

Note that the fact that
\bar{\mathcal{R}}^{(q;Q,\bar{Q})}_{(3;T,2)}
goes negative is not cause for concern, as there is a non-vanishing zeroth order term contributing, i.e.
\bar{\mathcal{R}}^{(q;Q,\bar{Q})}_{(3;0)}
, that should be combined with the result in Fig.
11
.
In Eq. (
121
),
\bar{\mathcal{R}}^{(q;Q,\bar{Q})}_{(3;T,2)}
is a prefactor in front of the transverse momentum diffusion correlator
\hat{\mathcal{F}}_{(T,2)}
defined in Eq. (
61
) and Eq. (
67
). The
y
-dependence of
\bar{\mathcal{R}}^{(q;Q,\bar{Q})}_{(3;T,2)}
is also explored in that figure. Recall that
\bar{\mathcal{R}}^{(q;Q,\bar{Q})}_{(3;T,2)}\propto\frac{1-\chi^{\prime}}{(1+\chi^{\prime})^{3}}
[Eq. (
109
)], where
\chi^{\prime}=\frac{M^{2}}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}}
, such at as
\ell_{2\perp}\gg M
,
\chi^{\prime}\to 0
, thus
\bar{\mathcal{R}}^{(q;Q,\bar{Q})}_{(3;T,2)}
becomes independent of
\ell_{2\perp}
and
M
.

The results in Fig.
11
complement the work in
Attems et al. 2023
;
Attems et al. 2024
, by exploring heavy-flavor production for highly virtual (anti)quarks interacting with Glauber (anti)quarks.
The increase in available phase space due to the presence of highly virtual quarks, as evidenced by the possibility of producing top quark–antiquark pairs in this context, underscores the need for a more comprehensive analysis. To accurately quantify the extent to which the quark–gluon plasma (QGP) enhances heavy-flavor production, it is essential to perform a Monte Carlo simulation that incorporates both our process and those outlined in
Attems et al. 2023
;
Attems et al. 2024
, in conjunction with a realistic hydrodynamical description of the QGP. Such a combined framework would allow for a systematic evaluation of medium effects on heavy-quark yields and provide a more reliable connection to experimental observables. The main ingredient missing for such an assessment to be complete is the contribution to heavy-quark pair production from gluon-gluon fusion, as alluded to earlier. A full Monte Carlo simulation should also analyze its results using energy-energy correlators
Barata et al. 2025
to track medium-induced heavy-quark pair production. Furthermore, no Monte Carlo simulation of jets in the QGP is complete without the associated photon production
Kumar and Vujanovic 2025
, to ensure that flavor hydrodynamization dynamics, which
\mathcal{K}_{i>1}
are sensitive to both herein and in
Kumar and Vujanovic 2025
, is accounted for in a manner consistent with data.

Conversion processes proceeding through
2\to 2
scattering have already been shown to affect the composition of jets in modern Monte Carlo event generators
Sirimanna et al. 2023
. The results in Ref.
Sirimanna et al. 2023
however did not include how conversion in
1\to 2
processes, specifically those explored in kernel-2 through kernel-4, affect the composition of jets. Thus, a follow-up study will examine how conversion processes in the QGP, both in
2\to 2
scattering as well as
1\to 2
in-medium radiative processes, affect parton composition of the jet at high-virtuality, through event generators such as MATTER
Majumder 2013a
;
Cao and Majumder 2020
, as well as low-virtuality, using event generators such as LBT and MARTINI.
15
15

15

The radiative energy loss in LBT
Cao et al. 2016
;
Cao et al. 2017
is based on the single-scattering induced emission, and accounts for heavy flavor energy loss. MARTINI uses on the Arnold, Moore, Yaffe formalism
Arnold et al. 2002
;
Jeon and Moore 2005
, including its next-to-leading order extensions and non-perturbative effects
Yazdi et al. 2022
;
Modarresi-Yazdi et al. 2025
. However, the Landau-Pomeranchuk-Migdal resummations for heavy quark energy loss has yet to be performed. A comparison of these two approches can be found in Ref.
Cao et al. 2017
.
Furthermore, as mentioned in our previous study
Kumar and Vujanovic 2025
, conversion processes are sensitive to the composition of the nuclear medium, specifically the dynamical generation and evolution of quarks as the nuclear medium transitions from early glasma-like dynamics to hydrodynamics, where the quark composition and occupation number reach their near thermal equilibrium levels. Thus, photon production and conversion processes in jet showering, within the higher-twist formalism, should be used simultaneously to probe flavor hydrodynamizaion dynamics in relativistic heavy-ion collisions.

As conversion processes in kernel-2 through kernel-4 were not included within the MATTER
Majumder 2013a
;
Cao and Majumder 2020
event generator used in a recent Bayesian constraint on
\hat{q}

Ehlers et al. 2025
. A follow-up Bayesian study can use conversion processes herein as an estimate of theoretical systematic uncertainties. Unlike Bayesian constraints of QGP viscosities, where an important form of theoretical systematic uncertainties has been taken into account
Everett et al. 2021
, such uncertainties have yet to be included in constraints of jet-medium transport coefficients, e.g.
\hat{q}
. Future Bayesian efforts aimed at improving our understanding of
\hat{q}
should include conversion processes as part of the high (and low) virtuality evolution of jet partons in the nuclear medium.

VI
Summary and Outlook

In this paper, an improved calculation of the scattering kernel for quark jet energy loss at NLO in a nuclear medium is presented. The contributions from both in-medium Glauber quark and gluon
Idilbi and Majumder 2009
have been accounted for the first time in a single uniform framework of the HT formalism that includes the effects of the heavy-quark masses, full phase factors, and fermion-to-boson conversion processes.

The virtual partons produced in the initial-state hard scattering are more likely to interact with gluons in the early stage, as the nuclear medium created in heavy-ion collisions is gluon-dominant. As the system transitions from the initial glasma dynamics to the hydrodynamical QGP, the light flavors hydrodynamize and jet partons start interacting with quarks in the nascent QGP, through channels in
\mathcal{K}_{2}
through
\mathcal{K}_{4}
. This changes the partonic composition of jets, relative to their vacuum composition or their composition in the glasma. The results presented herein, once implemented in a jet Monte Carlo, will allow to appreciate the change in the chemical composition of jets as the system hydrodynamizes.

The results presented herein thus account for interactions of highly virtual quarks with both quarks and gluons in the QGP. In so doing, all interactions with Glauber (anti)quarks and gluons have been accounted for within four distinct kernels. While kernel-1 deals with interactions between an incoming quark and Glauber gluons
Idilbi and Majumder 2009
, kernel-2 and -3 account for interactions with Glauber antiquarks, while kernel-4 accounts for interactions with Glauber quarks. The four kernels thus account for the following medium-induced radiative processes: (i)
q\to q+g
, (ii)
q\to g+g
, (iii)
q\to q+\bar{q}^{\prime}
as well as
q\to q^{\prime}+\bar{q}^{\prime}
, where
q^{\prime}
may be a different quark flavor from
q
, and (iv)
q\to q+q^{\prime}
, where
q^{\prime}
may be a different quark flavor than
q
once more. Furthermore, the quark mass scales were taken into account for all the relevant cases, namely, assuming that the QGP does not contain any charm or heavier quarks. Should relativistic heavy-ion collisions ever reach temperatures that allow for charm-quark (or even bottom-quark) thermalization in the QGP, as has been explored in lattice QCD calculations
Weber et al. 2021
;
Bazavov et al. 2014
, the results herein should be revised accordingly.

As was the case in our previous calculation
Kumar and Vujanovic 2025
, the phase factors
\left[2-2\cos\left\{\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}\zeta^{-}\right\}\right]
and
e^{-i\Delta z^{-}\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{M}}
that contain explicit dependence on
\ell_{2\perp}
have been kept when defining all scattering kernels. Furthermore, the basic assumption of the Glauber parton
Idilbi and Majumder 2009
having transverse momentum greater than its light-cone momenta, i.e.
k_{\perp}\gg k^{-},k^{+}
, is used throughout, and all processes involving a Glauber quark are found to be suppressed by
1/q^{-}
or its higher powers. The scattering kernels
\mathcal{S}_{i}
are presented before their Taylor expansion in
k
is performed, specifically around
k_{\perp}=0
and
k^{-}=0
. In addition to obtaining the complete next-to-leading order (NLO)
O\left(\alpha^{2}_{s}\right)
and next-to-leading-twist (NLT) scattering kernels, the non-perturbative operators, i.e.,
\hat{\mathcal{A}}_{i}
and
\hat{\mathcal{F}}_{i}
are linked to transverse-momentum-dependent parton distribution functions (TMD-PDFs).

Phenomenological studies by the JET and JETSCAPE Collaborations, together with estimates of
\hat{q}
in cold nuclear matter, indicate that
\hat{q}
is an order of magnitude larger in hot QGP than in cold nuclear matter
Burke et al. 2014
;
Ehlers et al. 2025
;
Deng and Wang 2010
;
Ru et al. 2021
. Assuming this trend remains the same for jet transport coefficients
\hat{\mathcal{F}}_{i}
, kernels 2–4 are expected to play a more significant role for jets traversing QGP than in cold nuclear environments.

For these scattering kernels to be implemented within realistic Monte Carlo simulations of jet partons in the QGP, the transport coefficients
\hat{\mathcal{A}}_{i}
and
\hat{\mathcal{F}}_{i}
need to be computed, either in finite-temperature field theory or using lattice gauge theory.
Having access to the scattering kernels herein, together with those in
Kumar and Vujanovic 2025
, allows to use photons and jets together when constraining flavor hydrodynamization dynamics. Still missing is a scattering kernel producing a virtual initial-state gluon, stemming from gluon-gluon fusion diagrams and quark-antiquark anihilation diagrams at the onset of the jet shower. Such diagrams are to be considered next, especially pertaining to the heavy-quark pair-production, which have become interesting
Barata et al. 2025
.

Data Availability

No data were created or analyzed in this study.

Appendix A
​​​​​​: List of variables

\kappa=\frac{1}{1+(1-y)^{2}}.

(122)

\eta=\frac{k^{-}}{yq^{-}}.

(123)

J_{1}=\left[\left(1+\eta y\right)\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-y\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right]^{2}+y^{2}M^{2}.

(124)

J_{2}=\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}\left\{-1+y-\eta y\left(1-y+\eta y\right)\right\}+y\boldsymbol{k}^{2}_{\perp}\left\{-1+y-\eta y\right\}+\left(\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}\right)\left\{1-y^{2}+2\eta y+\eta^{2}y^{2}\right\}.

(125)

\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}=p^{+}_{2}+\frac{\boldsymbol{p}^{2}_{2\perp}}{2(q^{-}-p^{-}_{2})}=\frac{\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)^{2}}{2y\left(1-y+\eta y\right)\left(1-\eta\right)q^{-}}.

(126)

\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{M}=p^{+}_{2}+\frac{\boldsymbol{p}^{2}_{2\perp}}{2\left(q^{-}-p^{-}_{2}\right)}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}=\frac{\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)^{2}+y^{2}\left(1-\eta\right)^{2}M^{2}}{2y\left(1-y+\eta y\right)\left(1-\eta\right)q^{-}}.

(127)

\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{0}=\ell^{+}_{2}+\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}}{2(q^{-}-\ell^{-}_{2})}=\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}}{2y(1-y)q^{-}}.

(128)

\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}=\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+y^{2}M^{2}}{2y(1-y)q^{-}}.

(129)

\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2},k)}_{M}=\ell^{+}_{2}+\frac{\boldsymbol{p}^{2}_{2\perp}+M^{2}}{2p^{-}_{2}}-\frac{\boldsymbol{k}^{2}_{\perp}+M^{2}}{2(1+\eta y)q^{-}}=\frac{J_{1}}{2(1+\eta y)y(1-y+\eta y)q^{-}}.

(130)

\mathcal{G}^{(\ell,k)}_{1}=\frac{\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}_{\perp}(1+\eta y)+(1-y)\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right]^{2}+y^{2}M^{2}(1+\eta)^{2}}{2y(1-y)q^{-}(1+\eta)(1+\eta y)}.

(131)

\mathcal{G}^{(\ell,k)}_{2}=\frac{\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{\perp}+\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)^{2}+y^{2}M^{2}(1+\eta)^{2}}{2y(1-y-\eta y)(1+\eta)q^{-}}.

(132)

\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{M}=\ell^{+}_{2}+p^{+}_{2}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}=\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}-yM^{2}}{2yq^{-}}+\frac{(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp})^{2}+M^{2}}{2q^{-}(1-y+\eta y)}.

(133)

\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{0}=\ell^{+}_{2}+p^{+}_{2}=\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}}{2yq^{-}}+\frac{(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp})^{2}}{2(1-y+\eta y)q^{-}}.

(134)

\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{1}=\ell^{+}_{2}+p^{+}_{2}=\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+M^{2}}{2yq^{-}}+\frac{(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp})^{2}+M^{2}}{2(1-y+\eta y)q^{-}}.

(135)

\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{2}=\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+M^{2}}{2(1-y)q^{-}}+\frac{(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}+\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp})^{2}}{2(1+\eta)yq^{-}}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}.

(136)

Appendix B
​​​​​​: Single-emission single-scattering kernel: one gluon and one quark in the final state

In this section, we summarize the calculation of all possible diagrams at next-to-leading order (NLO) contributing to kernel-1 with a gluon and a quark in the final state. We discuss singularity structure, contour integrations, and involved traces in the final calculation of the hadronic tensor.

Figure 12
:
Interference diagrams in which the radiated gluon undergoes double-gluon scattering, contributing to kernel-1.

B.1
Figure
4
(a) left and right cuts

Figure
12
represents a forward scattering diagram contributing to kernel-1. The left-cut give rise to an interference between the single-gluon emission with no scattering process and single gluon emission with double in-medium gluon scattering. The hadronic tensor for the left-cut diagram (Fig.
12
) is given by

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{1,\ell}&=\sum_{f}e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d^{4}x_{1}d^{4}x_{2}d^{4}z_{2}d^{4}z_{3}\int\frac{d^{4}\ell}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}p^{\prime}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}\ell_{2}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}p_{2}}{(2\pi)^{4}}e^{-ip^{\prime}x_{2}}e^{i\left(-q+\ell+p_{2}\right)x_{1}}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(x_{2})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(x_{1})\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times e^{i\left(q+p^{\prime}-p_{2}-\ell_{2}\right)z_{3}}e^{i(\ell_{2}-\ell)z_{2}}\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|A^{+c}(z_{3})A^{+d}(z_{2})\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle(i)^{2}f^{bed}f^{abc}{\rm Tr}\left[t^{a}t^{e}\right]g^{\sigma_{2}\rho_{2}}g^{\sigma_{1}\rho_{1}}\left(\ell^{-}_{2}+\ell^{-}\right)^{2}\\
&\times\frac{{\rm Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\mu}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}+M\right)\gamma^{\alpha_{1}}\left(\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\alpha_{2}}\left(\not{\ell}+\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\nu}\right]}{\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(\ell+p_{2}\right)^{2}-M^{2}+i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}-p_{2}\right)^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\ell_{2}^{2}-i\epsilon\right]}\\
&\times d^{\left(q+p^{\prime}-p_{2}\right)}_{\sigma_{1}\alpha_{1}}d^{(\ell_{2})}_{\sigma_{2}\rho_{1}}d^{(\ell)}_{\alpha_{2}\rho_{2}}(2\pi)\delta\left(\ell^{2}\right)(2\pi)\delta\left(p^{2}_{2}-M^{2}\right).\end{split}

(137)

The above expression has a singularity when the denominator of the propagator for
\ell_{2}
,
\ell^{\prime}
and
p_{1}
becomes on-shell. There is one pole for the momentum variable
\ell^{+}_{2}
and two poles for
p^{\prime+}
. The contour integration for
\ell_{2}^{+}
gives

C_{1}=\oint\frac{d\ell^{+}_{2}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{-i\ell^{+}_{2}(z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2})}}{[\ell^{2}_{2}-i\epsilon]}=\oint\frac{d\ell^{+}_{2}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{-i\ell^{+}_{2}(z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2})}}{2\ell^{-}_{2}\left[\ell^{+}_{2}-\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}}{2\ell^{-}_{2}}-i\epsilon\right]}=\frac{(-2\pi i)}{2\pi}\frac{\theta(z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2})}{2\ell^{-}_{2}}e^{-i\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}}{2\ell^{-}_{2}}\right)(z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2})}.

(138)

Similarly, the contour integration for
p^{\prime+}
gives

\begin{split}C_{2}&=\oint\frac{dp^{\prime+}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{-ip^{\prime+}\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}}{\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}-p_{2}\right)^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]}\\
&=\frac{(-2\pi i)}{2\pi}\frac{\theta\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}{4q^{-}\ell^{-}}e^{i\left(q^{+}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right)\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}\left[\frac{-1+e^{-i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell)}_{M}\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}}{\mathcal{G}^{(\ell)}_{M}}\right],\end{split}

(139)

where
\mathcal{G}^{(\ell)}_{M}
is defined in Eq. (
129
).

The trace in the numerator of the third line of Eq. (
137
) gives

\begin{split}&{\rm Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\mu}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}+M\right)\gamma^{\alpha_{1}}\left(\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\alpha_{2}}\left(\not{\ell}+\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\nu}\right]d^{(q+p^{\prime}-p_{2})}_{\sigma_{1}\alpha_{1}}d^{(\ell_{2})}_{\sigma_{2}\rho_{1}}d^{(\ell)}_{\alpha_{2}\rho_{2}}g^{\sigma_{2}\rho_{2}}g^{\sigma_{1}\rho_{1}}\\
&=\frac{8q^{-}[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]}{y(1-y)}\left[\frac{1+\left(1-y\right)^{2}}{y}\right]\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{\perp}+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}\right],\end{split}

(140)

where
\kappa
is defined in Eq. (
122
). Finally, the hadronic tensor for the left-cut diagram (Fig.
12
) reduces to the following form:

\displaystyle W^{\mu\nu}_{1,\ell}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle C_{A}\sum_{f}2\left[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}\right]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{i\left(q^{+}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right)\Delta x^{-}}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle

(141)

\displaystyle\times

\displaystyle n\int d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i\Delta z^{-}\mathcal{H}^{(\ell,p_{2})}_{2}}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}

\displaystyle\times

\displaystyle\int d\zeta^{-}\theta(z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2})\theta(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})\left[\frac{1+\left(1-y\right)^{2}}{y}\right]\left[\frac{\left(1+\frac{\eta}{2}\right)^{2}}{\left(1+\eta\right)}\right]\frac{\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{\perp}+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}\right]}{\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{\perp}+y^{2}M^{2}\right]^{2}}

\displaystyle\times

\displaystyle\left[1-e^{-i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell)}_{M}\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}\right]e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell)}_{M}\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)}\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|{\rm Tr}\left[A^{+}(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp})A^{+}(\zeta^{-},0)\right]\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle,

where
\mathcal{H}^{(\ell,p_{2})}_{2}
is defined in Eq. (
136
) and
\mathcal{G}^{(\ell)}_{M}
is defined in Eq. (

).
In above Eq. (
141
), the factor

originates from the time ordering of the two gluons originating from the plasma. Since the left-cut diagram [Fig.
12
] gives rise to two gluons on the same side of the cut-line, it imposes a phase space constraint
\theta(\Delta z^{+})
in the calculation of the hadonic tensor. The effective integral over
d(\Delta z^{+})
becomes

\displaystyle\int d(\Delta z^{+})\delta(\Delta z^{+})\theta(\Delta z^{+})=\theta\left(\Delta z^{+}=0\right)=n.

(142)

This integral arises when we perform phase space integration over
d(\Delta z^{+})
, such as shown in Eq. (
31
). In this paper, we define the integral shown in Eq. (

) as
n
, which ranges between 0 and 1, depending on the definition of
\theta(0)
. A detailed discussion can be found in Appendix A of Ref.
Sirimanna et al. 2022
.

The right-cut diagram shown in Fig.
12
is a complex conjugate of the left-cut diagram. The final expression of the hadronic tensor for the right-cut diagram reduces to the following form:

\displaystyle W^{\mu\nu}_{1,r}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle C_{A}\sum_{f}2\left[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}\right]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{i\left(q^{+}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right)\Delta x^{-}}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle

(143)

\displaystyle\times

\displaystyle\int d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell^{\prime}_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i\Delta z^{-}\mathcal{H}^{(\ell^{\prime},p_{2})}_{2}}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}

\displaystyle\times

\displaystyle n\int d\zeta^{-}\theta(z^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})\theta(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})\left[\frac{1+\left(1-y\right)^{2}}{y}\right]\left[\frac{\left(1+\frac{\eta}{2}\right)^{2}}{\left(1+\eta\right)}\right]\frac{\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\prime 2}_{\perp}+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}\right]}{\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\prime 2}_{\perp}+y^{2}M^{2}\right]^{2}}

\displaystyle\times

\displaystyle\left[1-e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell^{\prime})}_{M}\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)}\right]e^{-i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell^{\prime})}_{M}\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|{\rm Tr}\left[A^{+}\left(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp}\right)A^{+}\left(\zeta^{-},0\right)\right]\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle,

where
\mathcal{H}^{(\ell^{\prime},p_{2})}_{2}
is defined in Eq. (

) and
\mathcal{G}^{(\ell^{\prime})}_{M}
is defined in Eq. (

). The factor

in Eq. (
143

) and Eq. (
143
), respectively, gives the following expression of the hadronic tensor:

\displaystyle W^{\mu\nu}_{1,\ell+r}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle C_{A}\sum_{f}2\left[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}\right]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{i\left(q^{+}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right)\Delta x^{-}}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle

(144)

\displaystyle\times

\displaystyle n\int d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i\Delta z^{-}\mathcal{H}^{(\ell,p_{2})}_{2}}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}

\displaystyle\times

\displaystyle\int d\zeta^{-}\theta(\zeta^{-})\left[\frac{1+\left(1-y\right)^{2}}{y}\right]\left[\frac{\left(1+\frac{\eta}{2}\right)^{2}}{\left(1+\eta\right)}\right]\frac{\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{\perp}+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}\right]}{\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{\perp}+y^{2}M^{2}\right]^{2}}\left[\cos\left\{\mathcal{G}^{(\ell)}_{M}\zeta^{-}\right\}-1\right]

\displaystyle\times

\displaystyle\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|{\rm Tr}\left[A^{+}\left(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp}\right)A^{+}\left(\zeta^{-},0\right)\right]\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle.

(a)

:
Scattering diagrams with gluon in-medium scattering, contributing to kernel-1. (a) Gluon scattering with the medium occurs after gluon radiation. (b) Gluon scattering with the medium occurs before gluon radiation.

B.2
Figure

(b) and

Figure
13
identifies all position and momenta variables needed to compute the Feynman diagram in Figure
4
(b) and Figure
4
(c). The hadronic tensor for the central-cut diagram [Fig.
13
(a)] is

\displaystyle W^{\mu\nu}_{1,c}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{f}e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d^{4}x_{1}d^{4}x_{2}d^{4}z_{2}d^{4}z_{3}\int\frac{d^{4}p}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}p^{\prime}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}\ell_{2}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}p_{2}}{(2\pi)^{4}}e^{-ip^{\prime}x_{2}}e^{ipx_{1}}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(x_{2})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(x_{1})\right|P\right\rangle

(145)

\displaystyle\times

\displaystyle e^{iz_{3}\left(q+p^{\prime}-p_{2}-\ell_{2}\right)}e^{iz_{2}(\ell_{2}+p_{2}-q-p)}\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|{\rm Tr}\left[t^{d}A^{+c}t^{c}(z_{3})A^{+b}(z_{2})t^{b}t^{a}\right]\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle\delta^{ad}(2\pi)\delta\left(\ell_{2}^{2}\right)(2\pi)\delta\left(p_{2}^{2}-M^{2}\right)d^{(\ell_{2})}_{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{4}}

\displaystyle\times

\displaystyle\frac{{\rm Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\mu}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}+M\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{4}}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}-\not{\ell}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{-}\left(\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{-}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}-\not{\ell}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{1}}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}+M\right)\gamma^{\nu}\right]}{\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}-\ell_{2}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p-\ell_{2}\right)^{2}-M^{2}+i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p\right)^{2}-M^{2}+i\epsilon\right]}.

The above expression [Eq. (
145
)] becomes singular when the denominator of the quark propagator for
p_{1}
,
\ell
,
\ell^{\prime}
, and
p^{\prime}_{1}
vanishes. Computing this integral is easiest in the complex plane of
p^{+}
and
p^{\prime+}
, where both
p^{+}
and
p^{\prime+}
have two simple poles.
The contour integration for
p^{+}
can be carried out as

\begin{split}C_{1}&=\oint\frac{dp^{+}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{ip^{+}\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)}}{\left[\left(q+p\right)^{2}-M^{2}+i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p-\ell_{2}\right)^{2}-M^{2}+i\epsilon\right]}\\
&=\frac{(2\pi i)}{2\pi}\frac{\theta(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})}{4q^{-}(q^{-}-\ell^{-}_{2})}e^{i\left(-q^{+}+\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right)\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)}\left[\frac{-1+e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)}}{\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}}\right],\end{split}

(146)

where
\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}
is defined in Eq. (
129
).
The contour integration for
p^{\prime+}
proceeds analogously, giving

\begin{split}C_{2}&=\oint\frac{dp^{\prime+}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{-ip^{\prime+}\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}}{\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}-\ell_{2}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]}\\
&=\frac{(-2\pi i)}{2\pi}\frac{\theta\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}{4q^{-}(q^{-}-\ell^{-}_{2})}e^{-i\left(-q^{+}+\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right)\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}\left[\frac{-1+e^{-i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}}{\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}}\right].\end{split}

(147)

The trace in Eq. (
145
) can be simplified to get

\begin{split}&{\rm Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\mu}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}+M\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{4}}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}-\not{\ell}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{-}\left(\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{-}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}-\not{\ell}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{1}}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}+M\right)\gamma^{\nu}\right]d^{(\ell_{2})}_{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{4}}\\
&=32(q^{-})^{3}\left[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}\right]\left[\frac{1-y+\eta y}{y}\right]\left[\frac{1+\left(1-y\right)^{2}}{y}\right]\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}\right].\end{split}

(148)

The final expression of the hadronic tensor for the central-cut diagram [Fig.
13
(a)] becomes

\displaystyle\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{1,c}&=C_{F}\sum_{f}2\left[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}\right]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{i\Delta x^{-}\left(q^{+}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right)}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times\int d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i\Delta z^{-}\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{M}}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\\
&\times\int d\zeta^{-}\theta(\zeta^{-})\left[\frac{1+\left(1-y\right)^{2}}{y}\right]\frac{[\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}]}{\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+y^{2}M^{2}\right]^{2}}\left[2-2\cos\left\{\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}\zeta^{-}\right\}\right]\\
&\times\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|{\rm Tr}\left[A^{+}\left(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp}\right)A^{+}\left(\zeta^{-},0\right)\right]\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle,\end{split}

(149)

where
C_{F}=(N^{2}_{c}-1)/(2N_{c})
and
\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{M}
is defined in Eq. (
133
).

The left-cut [Fig.
13
(a)] gives rise to an interference between the single-gluon emission with no scattering process and single gluon pre-emission with double in-medium gluon scattering. The hadronic tensor for the left-cut diagram [Fig.
13
(a)] is given as

\displaystyle W^{\mu\nu}_{1,\ell}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{f}e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d^{4}x_{1}d^{4}x_{2}d^{4}z_{2}d^{4}z_{3}\int\frac{d^{4}\ell}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}p^{\prime}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}\ell_{2}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}p_{2}}{(2\pi)^{4}}e^{-ip^{\prime}x_{2}}e^{i(-q+\ell+\ell_{2})x_{1}}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(x_{2})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(x_{1})\right|P\right\rangle

(150)

\displaystyle\times e^{i\left(q+p^{\prime}-p_{2}-\ell_{2}\right)z_{3}}e^{i(p_{2}-\ell)z_{2}}\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|{\rm Tr}\left[t^{d}A^{+c}(z_{3})t^{c}A^{+b}(z_{2})t^{b}t^{a}\right]\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle\delta^{ad}d^{(\ell_{2})}_{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{4}}(2\pi)\delta\left(\ell_{2}^{2}\right)(2\pi)\delta\left(\ell^{2}-M^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\times\frac{{\rm Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\mu}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}+M\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{4}}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}-\not{\ell}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{-}\left(\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{-}\left(\not{\ell}+M\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{1}}\left(\not{\ell}_{2}+\not{\ell}+M\right)\gamma^{\nu}\right]}{\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}-\ell_{2}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[p_{2}^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(\ell_{2}+\ell\right)^{2}-M^{2}+i\epsilon\right]}.

Equation (
150
) admits singularities owing to the presence of two simple poles for momentum variable
p^{\prime+}
and one simple pole for
p^{+}_{2}
. The contour integration for momentum
p^{+}_{2}
gives

C_{1}=\oint\frac{dp^{+}_{2}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{-ip^{+}_{2}(z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2})}}{[p^{2}_{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon]}=\oint\frac{dp^{+}_{2}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{-ip^{+}_{2}(z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2})}}{2p^{-}_{2}\left[p^{+}_{2}-\frac{\boldsymbol{p}^{2}_{2\perp}+M^{2}}{2p^{-}_{2}}-i\epsilon\right]}=\frac{(-2\pi i)}{2\pi}\frac{\theta(z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2})}{2p^{-}_{2}}e^{-i\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{p}^{2}_{2\perp}+M^{2}}{2p^{-}_{2}}\right)(z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2})}.

(151)

Similarly, the contour integration for
p^{\prime+}
can be done

\begin{split}C_{2}&=\oint\frac{dp^{\prime+}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{-ip^{\prime+}(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}}{\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}-\ell_{2}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]}\\
&=\frac{(-2\pi i)}{2\pi}\frac{\theta(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}{4q^{-}(q^{-}-\ell^{-}_{2})}e^{i\left(q^{+}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}})(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}\left[\frac{-1+e^{-i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}}{\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}}\right],\end{split}

(152)

where
\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}
is defined in Eq. (
129
). The trace in the numerator of the third line of Eq. (
150
) yields

\begin{split}&{\rm Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\mu}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}+M\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{4}}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}-\not{\ell}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{-}\left(\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{-}\left(\not{\ell}+M\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{1}}\left(\not{\ell}_{2}+\not{\ell}+M\right)\gamma^{\nu}\right]d^{(\ell_{2})}_{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{4}}\\
&=32(q^{-})^{3}[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]\left[\frac{1-y+\eta y}{y}\right]\left[\frac{1+\left(1-y\right)^{2}}{y}\right]\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}\right].\end{split}

(153)

The final expression of the hadronic tensor for the left-cut diagram [Fig.
13
(a)] is given by

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{1,\ell}=&C_{F}\sum_{f}2[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{i\left(q^{+}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right)(\Delta x^{-})}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times n\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i(\Delta z^{-})\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{M}}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\theta(z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2})\theta(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})\\
&\times\left[\frac{1+\left(1-y\right)^{2}}{y}\right]\frac{\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}\right]}{\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+y^{2}M^{2}\right]^{2}}\left[1-e^{-i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}\right]e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})}\\
&\times\langle P_{A-1}|{\rm Tr}[A^{+}(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp})A^{+}(\zeta^{-},0)]|P_{A-1}\rangle,\end{split}

(154)

where
\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}
is defined in Eq. (
129
) and
\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{M}
is defined in Eq. (
133
). The factor
n
in Eq. (

) is described in Eq. (
142
).

The right-cut diagram shown in Fig.
13
(a) is a complex-conjugate of the left-cut diagram. The final expression of the hadronic tensor for the right-cut diagram [Fig.
13
(a)] reduces to the following form

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{1,r}=&C_{F}\sum_{f}2[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{i\left(q^{+}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right)(\Delta x^{-})}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times n\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i(\Delta z^{-})\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{M}}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\theta(-z^{-}_{3}+z^{-}_{2})\theta(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})\\
&\times\left[\frac{1+\left(1-y\right)^{2}}{y}\right]\frac{\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}\right]}{\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+y^{2}M^{2}\right]^{2}}\left[1-e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})}\right]e^{-i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}\\
&\times\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|{\rm Tr}\left[A^{+}\left(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp}\right)A^{+}\left(\zeta^{-},0\right)\right]\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle,\end{split}

where
\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}
is defined in Eq. (

) and
\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{M}
is defined in Eq. (

in Eq. (

). Adding the left-cut and right-cut diagrams, i.e., Eq. (

\displaystyle W^{\mu\nu}_{1,\ell+r}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle C_{F}\sum_{f}2[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{i\left(q^{+}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right)(\Delta x^{-})}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle

(156)

\displaystyle\times n\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i(\Delta z^{-})\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{M}}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\theta(\zeta^{-})

\displaystyle\times\left[\frac{1+\left(1-y\right)^{2}}{y}\right]\frac{\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}\right]}{\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+y^{2}M^{2}\right]^{2}}\left[\cos\left\{\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}\zeta^{-}\right\}-1\right]\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|{\rm Tr}\left[A^{+}\left(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp}\right)A^{+}\left(\zeta^{-},0\right)\right]\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle.

The hadronic tensor for the central-cut diagram shown in Fig.
13
(b) can be written as

\displaystyle W^{\mu\nu}_{1,c}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{f}e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d^{4}x_{1}d^{4}x_{2}d^{4}z_{2}d^{4}z_{3}\int\frac{d^{4}p}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}p^{\prime}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}\ell_{2}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}p_{2}}{(2\pi)^{4}}e^{-ip^{\prime}x_{2}}e^{ipx_{1}}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(x_{2})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(x_{1})\right|P\right\rangle

(157)

\displaystyle\times e^{i\left(q+p^{\prime}-p_{2}-\ell_{2}\right)z_{3}}e^{i(\ell_{2}+p_{2}-q-p)z_{2}}\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|{\rm Tr}\left[t^{c}A^{+c}(z_{3})t^{d}t^{a}t^{b}A^{+b}(z_{2})\right]\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle\delta^{ad}d^{(\ell_{2})}_{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{4}}(2\pi)\delta\left(\ell_{2}^{2}\right)(2\pi)\delta\left(p_{2}^{2}-M^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\times\frac{{\rm Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\mu}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}+M\right)\gamma^{-}\left(\not{\ell}_{2}+\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{4}}\left(\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{1}}\left(\not{\ell}_{2}+\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{-}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}+M\right)\gamma^{\nu}\right]}{\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(\ell_{2}+p_{2}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(\ell_{2}+p_{2}\right)^{2}-M^{2}+i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p\right)^{2}-M^{2}+i\epsilon\right]}.

Equation (
157
) has singularity arising from the denominator of the quark propagator with momentum
p_{1}
and
p^{\prime}_{1}
. We identify one pole for each momentum variable
p^{+}
and
p^{\prime+}
.
The contour integration for
p^{+}
in the complex plane is given by

C_{1}=\oint\frac{dp^{+}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{ip^{+}(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})}}{\left[\left(q+p\right)^{2}-M^{2}+i\epsilon\right]}=\oint\frac{dp^{+}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{ip^{+}(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})}}{2q^{-}[q^{+}+p^{+}-[M^{2}/(2q^{-})]+i\epsilon]}=\frac{(2\pi i)}{2\pi}\frac{\theta\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)}{2q^{-}}e^{i\left(-q^{+}+\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right)(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})}.

(158)

Similarly, the contour integration for momentum
p^{\prime+}
is carried out

C_{2}=\oint\frac{dp^{\prime+}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{-ip^{\prime+}(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}}{\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]}=\oint\frac{dp^{\prime+}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{-ip^{\prime+}(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}}{2q^{-}[q^{+}+p^{\prime+}-[M^{2}/(2q^{-})]-i\epsilon]}=\frac{(-2\pi i)}{2\pi}\frac{\theta\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}{2q^{-}}e^{i\left(q^{+}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right)(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}.

(159)

Including mass corrections, the trace yields

\begin{split}&{\rm Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\mu}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}+M\right)\gamma^{-}\left(\not{\ell}_{2}+\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{4}}\left(\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{1}}\left(\not{\ell}_{2}+\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{-}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}+M\right)\gamma^{\nu}\right]d^{(\ell_{2})}_{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{4}}\\
&=\frac{32[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}](q^{-})^{3}}{y\left(1-y+\eta y\right)}\left[\frac{\left(1+\eta y\right)^{2}+\left(1-y+\eta y\right)^{2}}{y}\right]\left[\left\{\left(1+\eta y\right)\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-y\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right\}^{2}+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}\right].\end{split}

(160)

The final expression of the hadronic tensor for the central-cut [Fig.
13
(b)] is

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{1,c}&=C_{F}\sum_{f}2[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{i\left(q^{+}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right)(\Delta x^{-})}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{M}(\Delta z^{-})}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\theta(\zeta^{-})\\
&\times\left[\frac{\left(1+\eta y\right)^{2}+\left(1-y+\eta y\right)^{2}}{y}\right]\frac{\left[\left\{\left(1+\eta y\right)\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-y\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right\}^{2}+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}\right]}{J_{1}^{2}}\\
&\times\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|{\rm Tr}\left[A^{+}\left(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp}\right)A^{+}\left(\zeta^{-},0\right)\right]\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle,\end{split}

(161)

where
\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{M}
is defined in Eq. (
133
).

(d) and

Figure 14
:
Scattering diagrams contributing to kernel-1. (a) The right-cut gives an interference between pre-emission scattering and post-emission scattering processes. The left-cut gives an interference between the gluon emission process in vacuum and the gluon emission with two in-medium gluon scattering. (b) Complex-conjugate of the processes on the left panel.

The hadronic tensor for the right-cut diagram shown in Fig.
14
(a) is

\displaystyle W^{\mu\nu}_{1,r_{1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{f}e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d^{4}x_{1}d^{4}x_{2}d^{4}z_{2}d^{4}z_{3}\int\frac{d^{4}p}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}p^{\prime}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}\ell_{2}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}p_{2}}{(2\pi)^{4}}e^{-ip^{\prime}x_{2}}e^{ipx_{1}}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(x_{2})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(x_{1})\right|P\right\rangle

(162)

\displaystyle\times e^{i\left(q+p^{\prime}-p_{2}-\ell_{2}\right)z_{3}}e^{i(\ell_{2}+p_{2}-q-p)z_{2}}\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|{\rm Tr}\left[t^{c}A^{+c}(z_{3})t^{d}t^{b}A^{+b}(z_{2})t^{a}\right]\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle\delta^{ad}d^{(\ell_{2})}_{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{4}}(2\pi)\delta\left(\ell_{2}^{2}\right)(2\pi)\delta\left(p_{2}^{2}-M^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\times\frac{{\rm Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\mu}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}+M\right)\gamma^{-}\left(\not{\ell}_{2}+\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{4}}\left(\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{-}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}-\not{\ell}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{1}}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}+M\right)\gamma^{\nu}\right]}{\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(\ell_{2}+p_{2}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p-\ell_{2}\right)^{2}-M^{2}+i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p\right)^{2}-M^{2}+i\epsilon\right]}.

) has singularity arising from the denominator of the quark propagator with momentum
p_{1}
,
\ell
and
p^{\prime}_{1}
. It has two simple poles for the momentum variable
p^{+}
and one simple pole for

. The contour integration for momentum

\begin{split}C_{1}&=\oint\frac{dp^{+}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{ip^{+}\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)}}{\left[\left(q+p\right)^{2}-M^{2}+i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p-\ell_{2}\right)^{2}-M^{2}+i\epsilon\right]}\\
&=\frac{(2\pi i)}{2\pi}\frac{\theta\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)}{4q^{-}(q^{-}-\ell^{-}_{2})}e^{i\left(-q^{+}+\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right)(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})}\left[\frac{-1+e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})}}{\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}}\right],\end{split}

(163)

where

(164)

&=\frac{32[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]\left(q^{-}\right)^{3}}{y}\left[\frac{1+\left(1-y\right)^{2}+\eta y(2-y)}{y}\right]\left[\left(1+\eta y\right)\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}-y\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}\right].\end{split}

The final expression of the hadronic tensor for the right-cut [Fig.

(a)] is given as

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{1,r_{1}}=&\left(C_{F}-\frac{C_{A}}{2}\right)\sum_{f}2[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{iq^{+}(\Delta x^{-})}e^{-i[M^{2}/(2q^{-})](\Delta x^{-})}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\

&\times\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|{\rm Tr}\left[A^{+}(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp})A^{+}(\zeta^{-},0)\right]\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle,\end{split}

where

),

), and

Now, we consider the left-cut diagram shown in Fig.
14
(a). Its hadronic tensor can be written as

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{1,\ell_{1}}&=\sum_{f}e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d^{4}x_{1}d^{4}x_{2}d^{4}z_{2}d^{4}z_{3}\int\frac{d^{4}\ell}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}p^{\prime}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}\ell_{2}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}p_{2}}{(2\pi)^{4}}e^{-ip^{\prime}x_{2}}e^{i(\ell_{2}+\ell-q)x_{1}}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(x_{2})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(x_{1})\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times e^{i\left(q+p^{\prime}-p_{2}-\ell_{2}\right)z_{3}}e^{i(p_{2}-\ell)z_{2}}\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|{\rm Tr}\left[t^{c}A^{+c}(z_{3})t^{d}t^{b}A^{+b}(z_{2})t^{a}\right]\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle\delta^{ad}d^{(\ell_{2})}_{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{4}}(2\pi)\delta\left(\ell_{2}^{2}\right)(2\pi)\delta\left(\ell^{2}-M^{2}\right)\\
&\times\frac{{\rm Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\mu}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}+M\right)\gamma^{-}\left(\not{\ell}_{2}+\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{4}}\left(\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{-}\left(\not{\ell}+M\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{1}}\left(\not{\ell}_{2}+\not{\ell}+M\right)\gamma^{\nu}\right]}{\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(\ell_{2}+p_{2}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[p^{2}_{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(\ell_{2}+\ell\right)^{2}-M^{2}+i\epsilon\right]}.\end{split}

(167)

Equation (
167
) has singularity arising from the denominator of the quark propagator with momentum

,

and
p_{2}
. We identify two simple poles for the momentum variable
p^{+}_{2}
and one simple pole for
p^{\prime+}
. We compute the integral in the complex plane of
p^{+}_{2}
and
p^{\prime+}
.
The contour integration for momentum
p^{\prime+}
is carried out as

C_{1}=\oint\frac{dp^{\prime+}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{-ip^{\prime+}(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}}{\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]}=\frac{(-2\pi i)}{2\pi}\frac{\theta\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}{2q^{-}}e^{i\left(q^{+}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right)(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}.

(168)

Similarly, the contour integration for momentum
p^{+}_{2}
is carried out as

\begin{split}C_{2}&=\oint\frac{dp^{+}_{2}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{-ip^{+}_{2}(z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2})}}{\left[p^{2}_{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(\ell_{2}+p_{2}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]}\\
&=\oint\frac{dp^{+}_{2}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{-ip^{+}_{2}(z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2})}}{\left[2p^{-}_{2}p^{+}_{2}-\left(\boldsymbol{p}^{2}_{2\perp}+M^{2}\right)-i\epsilon\right]\left[2\left(1+\eta y\right)q^{-}\left(\ell^{+}_{2}+p^{+}_{2}\right)-\left(\boldsymbol{k}^{2}_{\perp}+M^{2}\right)-i\epsilon\right]}\\
&=\frac{(-2\pi i)}{2\pi}\frac{\theta\left(z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2}\right)}{4p^{-}_{2}\left(1+\eta y\right)q^{-}}e^{-i\left[\frac{\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)^{2}+M^{2}}{2\left(1-y+\eta y\right)q^{-}}\right](z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2})}\left[\frac{1-e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2},k)}_{M}(z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2})}}{\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2},k)}_{M}}\right],\end{split}

(169)

where
\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2},k)}_{M}
is defined in Eq. (
130
). The trace in the numerator of the third line of Eq. (
167
) is the same as the trace for the right-cut diagram given in Eq. (
165
). The final expression of the hadronic tensor for the left-cut diagram [Fig.
14
(a)] is given by

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{1,\ell_{1}}&=\left(C_{F}-\frac{C_{A}}{2}\right)\sum_{f}2[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{i\left(q^{+}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right)(\Delta x^{-})}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times n\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i\Delta z^{-}\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{M}}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\theta\left(z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2}\right)\theta\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)\\
&\times\left[\frac{1+\left(1-y\right)^{2}+\eta y(2-y)}{y}\right]\frac{\left[\left(1+\eta y\right)\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}-y\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}\right]}{\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+y^{2}M^{2}\right]J_{1}}\left[-1+e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2},k)}_{M}(z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2})}\right]e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})}\\
&\times\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|{\rm Tr}\left[A^{+}(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp})A^{+}(\zeta^{-},0)\right]\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle,\end{split}

(170)

where
\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2},k)}_{M}
is defined in Eq. (
130
). The factor
n
in Eq. (
170
) is described in Eq. (
142
).

Subsequently, the diagram shown in the right panel of Fig.
14
is considered next. The topology of the diagram is the same as the diagram on the left panel. Moreover, they are complex conjugate of each other. The hadronic tensor for the left-cut diagram shown in Fig.
14
(b) can be written as

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{1,\ell_{2}}&=\left(C_{F}-\frac{C_{A}}{2}\right)\sum_{f}2[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{iq^{+}(\Delta x^{-})}e^{-i[M^{2}/(2q^{-})](\Delta x^{-})}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{M}(\Delta z^{-})}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\theta\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)\theta\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)\\
&\times\left[\frac{1+\left(1-y\right)^{2}+\eta y\left(2-y\right)}{y}\right]\frac{\left[\left(1+\eta y\right)\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}-y\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}\right]}{\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+y^{2}M^{2}\right]J_{1}}\left[-1+e^{-i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}\right]\\
&\times\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|{\rm Tr}\left[A^{+}\left(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp}\right)A^{+}\left(\zeta^{-},0\right)\right]\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle,\end{split}

(171)

where
\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}
is defined in Eq. (
129
),
\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{M}
defined in Eq. (
133
), and
J_{1}
is defined in Eq. (
124
).
Similarly, the final expression of the hadronic tensor for the right-cut diagram [Fig.
14
(b)] is given as

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{1,r_{2}}&=\left(C_{F}-\frac{C_{A}}{2}\right)\sum_{f}2[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{i\left(q^{+}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right)(\Delta x^{-})}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times n\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i(\Delta z^{-})\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{M}}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\theta\left(-z^{-}_{3}+z^{-}_{2}\right)\theta\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)\\
&\times\left[\frac{1+\left(1-y\right)^{2}+\eta y\left(2-y\right)}{y}\right]\frac{\left[\left(1+\eta y\right)\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}-y\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}\right]}{\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+y^{2}M^{2}\right]J_{1}}\left[-1+e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2},k)}_{M}(z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2})}\right]e^{-i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}\\
&\times\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|{\rm Tr}\left[A^{+}\left(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp}\right)A^{+}\left(\zeta^{-},0\right)\right]\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle,\end{split}

(172)

where
\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2},k)}_{M}
is defined in Eq. (
130
). The factor
n
in Eq. (
172
) is described in Eq. (
142
).

Note that our definition of the length-integration variable is
\zeta^{-}=x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}=x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}
which becomes
\zeta^{-}=-z^{-}_{2}=-z^{-}_{3}
when
x^{-}_{1}
and
x^{-}_{2}
are initialized as the origin. This leads the term
\left[-1+e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2},k)}_{M}(z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2})}\right]
in Eq. (
170
) and Eq. (
172
) to vanish. Therefore, adding the left-cut diagram [Fig.
14
(a)] and right-cut diagram [Fig.
14
(b)] one obtains zero, and hence no contribution to the energy-loss kernel. This implies the hadronic tensor for the left-cut diagram [Fig.
14
(a)] and right-cut diagram [Fig.
14
(b)] is

\displaystyle W^{\mu\nu}_{1,\ell_{1}}=W^{\mu\nu}_{1,r_{2}}=0.

(173)

However, the right-cut diagram [Fig.
14
(a)] and left-cut diagram [Fig.
14
(b)] have non-vanishing contributions, and their total hadronic tensor is given by

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{1,r_{1}+\ell_{2}}&=-\left(C_{F}-\frac{C_{A}}{2}\right)\sum_{f}2[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{iq^{+}(\Delta x^{-})}e^{-i[M^{2}/(2q^{-})](\Delta x^{-})}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{M}(\Delta z^{-})}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\theta\left(\zeta^{-}\right)\\
&\times\left[\frac{1+\left(1-y\right)^{2}+\eta y\left(2-y\right)}{y}\right]\frac{\left[\left(1+\eta y\right)\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}-y\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}\right]}{\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+y^{2}M^{2}\right]J_{1}}\left[2-2\cos\left\{\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}\zeta^{-}\right\}\right]\\
&\times\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|{\rm Tr}\left[A^{+}\left(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp}\right)A^{+}\left(\zeta^{-},0\right)\right]\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle.\end{split}

(174)

(f) and

A forward scattering diagram contributing to kernel-1.

The interference diagrams with a triple-gluon vertex are now considered, where variables needed to perform the calcultion, such as position and momentum, are labeled in Fig.
15
. The right-cut in the forward scattering diagram [Fig.
15
(a)] represents an interference between single-gluon emission with gluon-gluon in-medium scattering and single-gluon emission post quark-gluon scattering process. The hadronic tensor for the right-cut diagram [Fig.
15
(a)] is given as

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{1,r_{1}}&=\sum_{f}e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d^{4}x_{1}d^{4}x_{2}d^{4}z_{2}d^{4}z_{3}\int\frac{d^{4}p}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}p^{\prime}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}\ell_{2}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}p_{2}}{(2\pi)^{4}}e^{-ip^{\prime}x_{2}}e^{ipx_{1}}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(x_{2})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(x_{1})\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times e^{i\left(q+p^{\prime}-p_{2}-\ell_{2}\right)z_{3}}e^{i(\ell_{2}+p_{2}-q-p)z_{2}}\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|A^{+c}(z_{3})A^{+d}(z_{2})\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle(i)f^{bed}{\rm Tr}\left[t^{c}t^{b}t^{e}\right]g^{\sigma_{2}\rho_{2}}\left(-\ell^{-}_{2}-\ell^{-}\right)\\
&\times\frac{{\rm Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\mu}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}+M\right)\gamma^{-}\left(\not{\ell}_{2}+\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\alpha_{1}}\left(\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\alpha_{2}}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}+M\right)\gamma^{\nu}\right]}{\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(\ell_{2}+p_{2}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p\right)^{2}-M^{2}+i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p-p_{2}\right)^{2}+i\epsilon\right]}\\
&\times d^{\left(\ell_{2}\right)}_{\sigma_{2}\alpha_{1}}d^{(q+p-p_{2})}_{\alpha_{2}\rho_{2}}(2\pi)\delta\left(\ell^{2}_{2}\right)(2\pi)\delta\left(p^{2}_{2}-M^{2}\right).\end{split}

(175)

Equation (
175
) has singularity arising from the denominator of the quark propagator with momentum
p_{1}
,
\ell
and
p^{\prime}_{1}
. We identify one pole for momentum variable
p^{\prime+}
and two poles for
p^{+}
.
The contour integration for momentum
p^{+}
is given as

\begin{split}C_{1}&=\oint\frac{dp^{+}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{ip^{+}(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})}}{\left[\left(q+p\right)^{2}-M^{2}+i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p-p_{2}\right)^{2}+i\epsilon\right]}\\
&=\frac{(2\pi i)}{2\pi}\frac{\theta\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)}{4q^{-}\left(q^{-}-p^{-}_{2}\right)}e^{i\left[-q^{+}+\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right]\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)}\left[\frac{-1+e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{M}\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)}}{\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{M}}\right].\end{split}

(176)

Similarly, the contour integration for
p^{\prime+}
can be done

\begin{split}C_{2}=\oint\frac{dp^{\prime+}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{-ip^{\prime+}(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}}{\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]}=\frac{(-2\pi i)}{2\pi}\frac{\theta\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}{2q^{-}}e^{i\left(q^{+}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right)\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}.\end{split}

(177)

The trace in the numerator of the third line of Eq. (
175
) gives

\begin{split}&{\rm Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\mu}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}+M\right)\gamma^{-}\left(\not{\ell}_{2}+\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\alpha_{1}}\left(\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\alpha_{2}}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}+M\right)\gamma^{\nu}\right]d^{\left(\ell_{2}\right)}_{\sigma_{2}\alpha_{1}}d^{(q+p-p_{2})}_{\alpha_{2}\rho_{2}}g^{\sigma_{2}\rho_{2}}\\
&=\frac{16[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]\left(q^{-}\right)^{2}\left[1+\left(1-y\right)^{2}\right](1-y)}{(-1+\eta)y^{2}[1-y+\eta y]^{2}}\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)\cdot\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-y\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}\right].\end{split}

(178)

The final expression of the hadronic tensor for the right-cut diagram [Fig.
15
(a)] is given as

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{1,r_{1}}&=\frac{C_{A}}{2}\sum_{f}2[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{i\left(q^{+}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right)(\Delta x^{-})}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i(\Delta z^{-})\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{M}}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\theta(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})\theta(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})\\
&\times\left[\frac{1+(1-y)^{2}}{y}\right]\left[\frac{1-\frac{\eta}{2}}{1-\eta}\right]\frac{\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)\cdot\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-y\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}\right]}{\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)^{2}+y^{2}(1-\eta)^{2}M^{2}\right]J_{1}}\left[-1+e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{M}(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})}\right]\\
&\times\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|{\rm Tr}\left[A^{+}\left(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp}\right)A^{+}\left(\zeta^{-},0\right)\right]\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle,\end{split}

(179)

where
\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{M}
is defined in Eq. (
127
),
\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{M}
defined in Eq. (
133
), and
J_{1}
is defined in Eq. (
124
).

Furthermore, the left-cut diagram is shown in Fig.
15
(a). Its hadronic tensor can be written as

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{1,\ell_{1}}&=\sum_{f}e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d^{4}x_{1}d^{4}x_{2}d^{4}z_{2}d^{4}z_{3}\int\frac{d^{4}\ell}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}p^{\prime}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}\ell_{2}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}p_{2}}{(2\pi)^{4}}e^{-ip^{\prime}x_{2}}e^{i(\ell+p_{2}-q)x_{1}}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(x_{2})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(x_{1})\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times e^{i\left(q+p^{\prime}-p_{2}-\ell_{2}\right)z_{3}}e^{i(\ell_{2}-\ell)z_{2}}\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|A^{+c}(z_{3})A^{+d}(z_{2})\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle(i)f^{bed}{\rm Tr}\left[t^{c}t^{b}t^{e}\right]g^{\sigma_{2}\rho_{2}}\left(-\ell^{-}_{2}-\ell^{-}\right)\\
&\times\frac{{\rm Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\mu}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}+M\right)\gamma^{-}\left(\not{\ell}_{2}+\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\alpha_{1}}\left(\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\alpha_{2}}\left(\not{\ell}+\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\nu}\right]}{\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(\ell_{2}+p_{2}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\ell^{2}_{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[(\ell+p_{2})^{2}-M^{2}+i\epsilon\right]}\\
&\times d^{\left(\ell_{2}\right)}_{\sigma_{2}\alpha_{1}}d^{(\ell)}_{\alpha_{2}\rho_{2}}(2\pi)\delta\left(\ell^{2}\right)(2\pi)\delta\left(p^{2}_{2}-M^{2}\right).\end{split}

Equation (

) has singularity arising from the denominator of the quark propagator with momentum

&=\frac{(-2\pi i)}{2\pi}\frac{\theta\left(z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2}\right)}{4\ell^{-}_{2}\left(\ell^{-}_{2}+p^{-}_{2}\right)}e^{-i\left[\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}}{2\ell^{-}_{2}}\right]\left(z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2}\right)}\left[\frac{1-e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell,k)}_{1}\left(z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2}\right)}}{\mathcal{G}^{(\ell,k)}_{1}}\right],\end{split}

) gives

\begin{split}&{\rm Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\mu}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}+M\right)\gamma^{-}\left(\not{\ell}_{2}+\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\alpha_{1}}\left(\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\alpha_{2}}\left(\not{\ell}+\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\nu}\right]d^{\left(\ell_{2}\right)}_{\sigma_{2}\alpha_{1}}d^{(\ell)}_{\alpha_{2}\rho_{2}}g^{\sigma_{2}\rho_{2}}\\
&=\frac{16[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]\left(q^{-}\right)^{2}\left[1+\left(1-y\right)^{2}\right](1-y)}{(-1+\eta)y^{2}[1-y+\eta y]^{2}}\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}_{\perp}\cdot\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{\perp}+(1-y)\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}\right].\end{split}

The final expression of the hadronic tensor for the left-cut diagram [Fig.

(a)] is given as

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{1,\ell_{1}}&=-\frac{C_{A}}{2}\sum_{f}2[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{i\left(q^{+}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right)(\Delta x^{-})}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times n\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i(\Delta z^{-})\mathcal{H}^{(\ell,p_{2})}_{2}}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\theta\left(z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2}\right)\theta\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)\\

&\times\left[1-e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell,k)}_{1}(z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2})}\right]e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell)}_{M}(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})}\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|{\rm Tr}\left[A^{+}(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp})A^{+}(\zeta^{-},0)\right]\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle,\end{split}

),

Following this, we consider the diagram shown in the right panel of Fig.
15
. The topology of the diagram is the same as the diagram on the left panel. Moreover, they are complex conjugate of each other. The final expression of the hadronic tensor for the left-cut diagram shown in Fig.
15
(b) can be written as

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{1,\ell_{2}}&=\frac{C_{A}}{2}\sum_{f}2[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{i\left(q^{+}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right)(\Delta x^{-})}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i(\Delta z^{-})\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{M}}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\theta(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})\theta(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})\\
&\times\left[\frac{1+(1-y)^{2}}{y}\right]\left[\frac{1-\frac{\eta}{2}}{1-\eta}\right]\frac{\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)\cdot\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-y\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}\right]}{\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)^{2}+M^{2}y^{2}(1-\eta)^{2}\right]J_{1}}\left[-1+e^{-i\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{M}(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}\right]\\
&\times\left\langle P_{A-1}|{\rm Tr}[A^{+}(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp})A^{+}(\zeta^{-},0)]|P_{A-1}\right\rangle,\end{split}

(185)

where
\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{M}
is defined in Eq. (
127
),
\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{M}
defined in Eq. (
133
), and
J_{1}
is defined in Eq. (
124
).

Similarly, the hadronic tensor for the right-cut diagram in Fig.
15
(b) is given as

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{1,r_{2}}&=-\frac{C_{A}}{2}\sum_{f}2[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{i\left(q^{+}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right)(\Delta x^{-})}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times n\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell^{\prime}_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i(\Delta z^{-})\mathcal{H}^{(\ell^{\prime},p_{2})}_{2}}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\theta(-z^{-}_{3}+z^{-}_{2})\theta(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})\\
&\times\left[\frac{1+(1-y)^{2}}{y}\right]\left[1+\frac{\eta}{2}\right]\frac{\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\prime}_{\perp}\cdot\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\prime}_{\perp}+(1-y)\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}\right]}{\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\prime 2}_{\perp}+y^{2}M^{2}\right]\left[\left\{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{\prime}_{\perp}(1+\eta y)+(1-y)\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right\}^{2}+y^{2}M^{2}\right]}\\
&\times\left[1-e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell^{\prime},k)}_{1}(z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2})}\right]e^{-i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell^{\prime})}_{M}(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|{\rm Tr}\left[A^{+}(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp})A^{+}(\zeta^{-},0)\right]\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle,\end{split}

(186)

where
\mathcal{G}^{(\ell^{\prime},k)}_{1}
is defined in Eq. (
131
),
\mathcal{H}^{(\ell^{\prime},p_{2})}_{2}
defined in Eq. (
136
). The factor
n
in Eq. (

) is described in Eq. (
142
).

Note that our definition of the length integration variable is
\zeta^{-}=x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}=x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}
which becomes
\zeta^{-}=-z^{-}_{2}=-z^{-}_{3}
when
x^{-}_{1}
and
x^{-}_{2}
are initialized as the origin. This leads the term
\left[1-e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell,k)}_{1}(z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2})}\right]

) to vanish.
Therefore, the left-cut diagram [Fig.

(a)] and right-cut diagram [Fig.

\displaystyle W^{\mu\nu}_{1,\ell_{1}}=W^{\mu\nu}_{1,r_{2}}=0.

(187)

However, the right-cut diagram [Fig.

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{1,r_{1}+\ell_{2}}=&-\frac{C_{A}}{2}\sum_{f}2[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{i\left(q^{+}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right)(\Delta x^{-})}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i(\Delta z^{-})\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{M}}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\theta(\zeta^{-})\\
&\times\left[\frac{1+(1-y)^{2}}{y}\right]\left[\frac{1-\frac{\eta}{2}}{1-\eta}\right]\frac{\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)\cdot\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-y\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}\right]}{\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)^{2}+y^{2}(1-\eta)^{2}M^{2}\right]J_{1}}\left[2-2\cos\left\{\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{M}\zeta^{-}\right\}\right]\\
&\times\left\langle P_{A-1}|{\rm Tr}[A^{+}(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp})A^{+}(\zeta^{-},0)]|P_{A-1}\right\rangle.\end{split}

(188)

A forward scattering diagram contributing to kernel-1.

The forward scattering diagrams shown in Fig.
16
depicts the variables needed to perform the calcualtion. The center-cut [Fig.

(a)] leads to a diagram with gluon-gluon scattering on the amplitude side and post-emission quark scattering with the medium on the complex conjugate side. The hadronic tensor for the center-cut diagram [Fig.
16
(a)] is given as

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{1,c_{1}}&=\sum_{f}e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d^{4}x_{1}d^{4}x_{2}d^{4}z_{2}d^{4}z_{3}\int\frac{d^{4}p}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}p^{\prime}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}\ell_{2}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}p_{2}}{(2\pi)^{4}}e^{-ip^{\prime}x_{2}}e^{ipx_{1}}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(x_{2})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(x_{1})\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times e^{i\left(q+p^{\prime}-p_{2}-\ell_{2}\right)z_{3}}e^{i(\ell_{2}+p_{2}-q-p)z_{2}}\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|A^{+c}(z_{3})A^{+d}(z_{2})\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle(-i)f^{bed}{\rm Tr}\left[t^{b}t^{c}t^{e}\right]g^{\sigma_{2}\rho_{2}}\left(-\ell^{-}_{2}-\ell^{-}\right)\\
&\times\frac{{\rm Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\mu}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}+M\right)\gamma^{\alpha_{1}}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}-\not{\ell}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{-}\left(\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\alpha_{2}}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}+M\right)\gamma^{\nu}\right]}{\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}-\ell_{2}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p\right)^{2}-M^{2}+i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p-p_{2}\right)^{2}+i\epsilon\right]}\\
&\times d^{\left(\ell_{2}\right)}_{\sigma_{2}\alpha_{1}}d^{(q+p-p_{2})}_{\alpha_{2}\rho_{2}}(2\pi)\delta\left(\ell^{2}_{2}\right)(2\pi)\delta\left(p^{2}_{2}-M^{2}\right).\end{split}

(189)

The above expression of the hadonic tensor has singularity when the denominator of the propagator for
p_{1}
,
\ell
,
\ell^{\prime}
and
p^{\prime}_{1}
becomes on-shell. It contains two simple poles for
p^{+}
and
p^{\prime+}
. The contour integration for
p^{+}
gives

\begin{split}C_{1}&=\oint\frac{dp^{+}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{ip^{+}(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})}}{\left[\left(q+p\right)^{2}-M^{2}+i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p-p_{2}\right)^{2}+i\epsilon\right]}\\
&=\frac{(2\pi i)}{2\pi}\frac{\theta\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)}{4q^{-}(q^{-}-p^{-}_{2})}e^{i\left[-q^{+}+\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right]\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)}\left[\frac{-1+e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{M}\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)}}{\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{M}}\right],\end{split}

(190)

where
\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{M}
is defined in Eq. (
127
). Similarly, the contour integration for
p^{\prime+}
can be done

\begin{split}C_{2}&=\oint\frac{dp^{\prime+}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{-ip^{\prime+}\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}}{\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}-\ell_{2}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]}\\
&=\frac{(-2\pi i)}{2\pi}\frac{\theta\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}{4q^{-}(q^{-}-\ell^{-}_{2})}e^{-i\left(-q^{+}+\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right)\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}\left[\frac{-1+e^{-i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}}{\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}}\right],\end{split}

(191)

where
\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}
is defined in Eq. (
129
). The trace in the numerator of the third line of Eq. (

) gives

\begin{split}&{\rm Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\mu}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}+M\right)\gamma^{\alpha_{1}}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}-\not{\ell}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{-}\left(\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\alpha_{2}}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}+M\right)\gamma^{\nu}\right]d^{\left(\ell_{2}\right)}_{\sigma_{2}\alpha_{1}}d^{(q+p-p_{2})}_{\alpha_{2}\rho_{2}}g^{\sigma_{2}\rho_{2}}\\
&=-16[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}](1-y)^{2}\left(q^{-}\right)^{2}\left[\frac{1+\left(1-y\right)^{2}}{(1-\eta)y^{2}\left(1-y+\eta y\right)^{2}}\right]\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)\cdot\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}\right].\end{split}

(192)

The final expression of the hadronic tensor for center-cut diagram [Fig.

\displaystyle\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{1,c_{1}}&=-\frac{C_{A}}{2}\sum_{f}2\left[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}\right]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{i\Delta x^{-}\left(q^{+}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right)}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i\Delta z^{-}\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{M}}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\theta\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)\theta\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)\\
&\times\left[\frac{1+\left(1-y\right)^{2}}{y}\right]\left[\frac{\left(1-\frac{\eta}{2}\right)}{\left(1-\eta\right)}\right]\frac{\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)\cdot\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}\right]}{\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+y^{2}M^{2}\right]\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)^{2}+y^{2}\left(1-\eta\right)^{2}M^{2}\right]}\\
&\times\left[-1+e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{M}\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)}\right]\left[-1+e^{-i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}\right]\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|{\rm Tr}\left[A^{+}\left(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp}\right)A^{+}\left(\zeta^{-},0\right)\right]\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle.\end{split}

The final hadronic tensor for right-cut diagram [Fig.

\displaystyle\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{1,r}&=-\frac{C_{A}}{2}\sum_{f}2\left[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}\right]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{i\Delta x^{-}\left(q^{+}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right)}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times n\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i\Delta z^{-}\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{M}}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}e^{-i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}\theta(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})\\
&\times\left[\frac{1+\left(1-y\right)^{2}}{y}\right]\left[1-\frac{\eta}{2}\right]\frac{\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)\cdot\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}\right]}{\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+y^{2}M^{2}\right]\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)^{2}+y^{2}\left(1-\eta\right)^{2}M^{2}\right]}\\
&\times\left[\theta(-z^{-}_{3}+z^{-}_{2})+\theta(z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2})e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{M}(z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2})}+\theta(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{3})e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{M}(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})}\left\{-1+e^{-i\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{M}(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{3})}\right\}\right],\\
&\times\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|{\rm Tr}\left[A^{+}\left(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp}\right)A^{+}\left(\zeta^{-},0\right)\right]\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle,\end{split}

(199)

where
\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{M}
is defined in Eq. (
127
),
\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}
is defined in Eq. (
129
) and
\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{M}
is defined in Eq. (
133
). The factor
n
in Eq. (
199
) is described in Eq. (
142
). The left-cut and right-cut expressions given in Eq. (
197
) and Eq. (
199
) can be added together using
\zeta^{-}=x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}=x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}
and further initializing
x^{-}_{1}
and
x^{-}_{2}
to the origin, yielding

\displaystyle\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{1,\ell+r}&=-\frac{C_{A}}{2}\sum_{f}2\left[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}\right]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{i\Delta x^{-}\left(q^{+}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right)}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times n\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i\Delta z^{-}\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{M}}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\theta(\zeta^{-})\\
&\times\left[\frac{1+\left(1-y\right)^{2}}{y}\right]\left[1-\frac{\eta}{2}\right]\frac{\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)\cdot\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}\right]}{\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+y^{2}M^{2}\right]\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)^{2}+y^{2}\left(1-\eta\right)^{2}M^{2}\right]}\\
&\times\left[4\cos\left\{\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}\zeta^{-}\right\}-2\cos\left\{\left(\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{M}-\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}\right)\zeta^{-}\right\}\right]\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|{\rm Tr}\left[A^{+}\left(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp}\right)A^{+}\left(\zeta^{-},0\right)\right]\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle.\end{split}

(200)

Note that the left-cut diagram in Fig.
16
(a) is identical to the left-cut diagram in Fig.
16
(b). This is mainly because
\theta(-z^{-}_{3}+z^{-}_{2})
and
\theta(z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2})
in Eq. (
197
) account for both the possibility, i.e.
z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2}>0
and
z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2}<0
. Hence, it is not added as it would lead to double-counting. Similarly, the right-cut diagram in Fig.
16
(a) is identical to the right-cut diagram in Fig.
16
(b) and hence it would not be added.

(j) and
