(b)

Figure 17
:
A forward scattering diagram contributing to kernel-1 with a gluon emission post two successive gluon scattering.

The last diagram to consider in kernel-1 is presented in Fig.
17
, along with the variables used in the calculation. The cut diagram in Fig.
17
(a) contains final-state gluon radiation on the amplitude side and two successive gluon scatterings followed by the gluon radiation on the complex-conjugate side. The hadronic tensor for the diagram in Fig.
17
(a) is given by

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{1,\ell}&=\sum_{f}e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d^{4}x_{1}d^{4}x_{2}d^{4}z_{2}d^{4}z_{3}\int\frac{d^{4}\ell^{\prime}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}p^{\prime}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}\ell_{2}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}p_{2}}{(2\pi)^{4}}e^{-ip^{\prime}x_{2}}e^{i(\ell_{2}+p_{2}-q)x_{1}}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(x_{2})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(x_{1})\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times e^{i\left(q+p^{\prime}-\ell^{\prime}\right)z_{3}}e^{i(\ell^{\prime}-\ell_{2}-p_{2})z_{2}}\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|A^{+c}(z_{3})A^{+b}(z_{2})\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle{\rm Tr}\left[t^{c}t^{b}t^{d}t^{a}\right]\delta^{ad}\\
&\times\frac{{\rm Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\mu}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}+M\right)\gamma^{-}\left(\not{\ell}^{\prime}+M\right)\gamma^{-}\left(\not{\ell}_{2}+\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{4}}\left(\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{1}}\left(\not{\ell}_{2}+\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\nu}\right]}{\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(\ell^{\prime}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(\ell_{2}+p_{2}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(\ell_{2}+p_{2}\right)^{2}-M^{2}+i\epsilon\right]}\\
&\times d^{\left(\ell_{2}\right)}_{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{4}}(2\pi)\delta\left(\ell^{2}_{2}\right)(2\pi)\delta\left(p^{2}_{2}-M^{2}\right).\end{split}

(201)

Equation (
201
) has singularity arising from the denominator of the quark propagator with momentum
\ell^{\prime}
and
p^{\prime}_{1}
. We identify one pole for each momentum variable
p^{\prime+}
and
\ell^{\prime+}
.
The contour integration for momentum
p^{\prime+}
is given as

\begin{split}C_{1}=\oint\frac{dp^{\prime+}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{-ip^{\prime+}(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}}{\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]}=\frac{(-2\pi i)}{2\pi}\frac{\theta\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}{2q^{-}}e^{i\left(q^{+}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right)\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}.\end{split}

(202)

Similarly, the contour integration for
\ell^{\prime+}
can be done giving:

\begin{split}C_{2}&=\oint\frac{d\ell^{\prime+}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{-i\ell^{\prime+}(z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2})}}{\left[\ell^{\prime 2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]}=\frac{(-2\pi i)}{2\pi}\frac{\theta\left(z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2}\right)}{2\ell^{\prime-}}e^{-i\left[\frac{\boldsymbol{k}^{2}_{\perp}+M^{2}}{2\ell^{\prime-}}\right]\left(z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2}\right)}.\end{split}

(203)

The trace in the numerator of the third line of Eq. (
201
) gives

\begin{split}&{\rm Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\mu}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}+M\right)\gamma^{-}\left(\not{\ell}^{\prime}+M\right)\gamma^{-}\left(\not{\ell}_{2}+\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{4}}\left(\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{1}}\left(\not{\ell}_{2}+\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\nu}\right]d^{\left(\ell_{2}\right)}_{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{4}}\\
&=\frac{32[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}](1+\eta y)\left(q^{-}\right)^{3}}{(1-y)y}\left[\frac{1+\left(1-y\right)^{2}}{y}\right]\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}\right].\end{split}

(204)

The final hadronic tensor for the diagram in Fig.
17
(a) is given by

\displaystyle\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{1,\ell}&=-C_{F}\sum_{f}2\left[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}\right]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{i\Delta x^{-}\left(q^{+}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right)}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times n\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i\Delta z^{-}\left\{\frac{\boldsymbol{k}^{2}_{\perp}+M^{2}}{2q^{-}(1+\eta y)}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right\}}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\theta(z^{-}_{3}-z^{-}_{2})\theta(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})\\
&\times\left[\frac{1+\left(1-y\right)^{2}}{y}\right]\frac{\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}\right]}{\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+y^{2}M^{2}\right]^{2}}e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)}\\
&\times\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|{\rm Tr}\left[A^{+}\left(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp}\right)A^{+}\left(\zeta^{-},0\right)\right]\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle.\end{split}

(205)

The factor
n
in Eq. (
205
) is described in Eq. (
142
). Note, the diagram shown in Fig.
17
(b) is a complex-conjugate of the diagram in Fig.
17
(a). The final expression of the hadronic tensor for Fig.
17
(b) is given as

\displaystyle\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{1,r}&=-C_{F}\sum_{f}2\left[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}\right]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{i\Delta x^{-}\left(q^{+}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right)}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times n\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i\Delta z^{-}\left\{\frac{\boldsymbol{k}^{2}_{\perp}+M^{2}}{2q^{-}(1+\eta y)}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right\}}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\theta(-z^{-}_{3}+z^{-}_{2})\theta(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})\\
&\times\left[\frac{1+\left(1-y\right)^{2}}{y}\right]\frac{\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}\right]}{\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+y^{2}M^{2}\right]^{2}}e^{-i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|{\rm Tr}\left[A^{+}\left(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp}\right)A^{+}\left(\zeta^{-},0\right)\right]\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle.\end{split}

(206)

Adding the hadronic tensors of the forward scattering diagrams in Fig.
17
(a) and Fig.
17
(b) gives

\displaystyle\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{1,\ell+r}&=-C_{F}\sum_{f}2\left[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}\right]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{i\Delta x^{-}\left(q^{+}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right)}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times n\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i\Delta z^{-}\left\{\frac{\boldsymbol{k}^{2}_{\perp}+M^{2}}{2q^{-}(1+\eta y)}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right\}}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\theta(\zeta^{-})\\
&\times\left[\frac{1+\left(1-y\right)^{2}}{y}\right]\frac{\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}\right]}{\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+y^{2}M^{2}\right]^{2}}\cos\left\{\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{M}\zeta^{-}\right\}\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|{\rm Tr}\left[A^{+}\left(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp}\right)A^{+}\left(\zeta^{-},0\right)\right]\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle,\end{split}

(207)

where the factor
n
is defined in Eq. (

). Notice that the third line in Eq. (

) does not depend on
\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}
and
k^{-}
, therefore, the diagrams in Fig.

(a) and Fig.

Appendix C
​​​​​​: Single-emission single-scattering kernel: Two gluons in the final state

In this section, we summarize the calculation of all possible diagrams at next-leading-order (NLO) contributing to kernel-2 with two gluons in the final state. We discuss singularity structure, contour integrations, and involved traces in the final calculation of the hadronic tensor.

(a)

Quark anti-quark scattering channel.

(b)

Quark anti-quark annihilation diagram.

Figure 18
:
A forward scattering diagram contributing to kernel-2.

C.1
Figure
6
(a) and
6
(b)

The hadronic tensor for Fig.
18
(a) has the following form

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{2,c}&=\sum_{f}e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d^{4}x_{1}d^{4}x_{2}d^{4}z_{2}d^{4}z_{3}\int\frac{d^{4}p}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}p^{\prime}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}\ell_{2}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}p_{2}}{(2\pi)^{4}}e^{-ip^{\prime}x_{2}}e^{ipx_{1}}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(x_{2})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(x_{1})\right|P\right\rangle\delta^{ad}\delta^{bc}\\
&\times e^{i\left(q+p^{\prime}-p_{2}-\ell_{2}\right)z_{3}}e^{i(\ell_{2}+p_{2}-q-p)z_{2}}\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(z_{2})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(z_{3})\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle(2\pi)\delta\left(\ell_{2}^{2}\right)(2\pi)\delta\left(p_{2}^{2}\right){\rm Tr}[t^{d}t^{c}t^{b}t^{a}]\\
&\times\frac{{\rm Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\mu}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{4}}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}-\not{\ell}_{2}\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{3}}\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\sigma_{2}}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}-\not{\ell}_{2}\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{1}}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}\right)\gamma^{\nu}\right]}{\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}\right)^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p\right)^{2}+i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}-\ell_{2}\right)^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p-\ell_{2}\right)^{2}+i\epsilon\right]}d^{(\ell_{2})}_{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{4}}d^{(p_{2})}_{\sigma_{3}\sigma_{2}}.\end{split}

(208)

The above expression of the hadonic tensor has singularity when the denominator of the propagator for
p_{1}
,
\ell
,
\ell^{\prime}
and
p^{\prime}_{1}
becomes on-shell. It contains two simple poles for
p^{+}
and
p^{\prime+}
. The contour integration for
p^{+}
gives

\begin{split}C_{1}&=\oint\frac{dp^{+}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{ip^{+}(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})}}{\left[\left(q+p\right)^{2}+i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p-\ell_{2}\right)^{2}+i\epsilon\right]}\\
&=\oint\frac{dp^{+}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{ip^{+}(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})}}{2q^{-}[q^{+}+p^{+}+i\epsilon]2(q^{-}-\ell^{-}_{2})\left[q^{+}+p^{+}-\ell^{+}_{2}-\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}}{2(q^{-}-\ell^{-}_{2})}+i\epsilon\right]}\\
&=\frac{(2\pi i)}{2\pi}\frac{\theta(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})}{4q^{-}(q^{-}-\ell^{-}_{2})}e^{-iq^{+}(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})}\left[\frac{-1+e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{0}(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})}}{\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{0}}\right],\end{split}

(209)

where
\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{0}
is defined in Eq. (
128
). Similarly, the contour integration for
p^{\prime+}
gives

\begin{split}C_{2}&=\oint\frac{dp^{\prime+}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{-ip^{\prime+}(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}}{\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}\right)^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}-\ell_{2}\right)^{2}-i\epsilon\right]}\\
&=\oint\frac{dp^{\prime+}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{-ip^{\prime+}(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}}{2q^{-}[q^{+}+p^{\prime+}-i\epsilon]2(q^{-}-\ell^{-}_{2})\left[q^{+}+p^{\prime+}-\ell^{+}_{2}-\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}}{2(q^{-}-\ell^{-}_{2})}-i\epsilon\right]}\\
&=\frac{(-2\pi i)}{2\pi}\frac{\theta(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}{4q^{-}(q^{-}-\ell^{-}_{2})}e^{iq^{+}(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}\left[\frac{-1+e^{-i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{0}(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}}{\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{0}}\right].\end{split}

(210)

The trace in the third line of Eq. (
208
) simplifies to

\begin{split}&{\rm Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\mu}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{4}}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}-\not{\ell}_{2}\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{3}}\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\sigma_{2}}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}-\not{\ell}_{2}\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{1}}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}\right)\gamma^{\nu}\right]d^{(\ell_{2})}_{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{4}}d^{(p_{2})}_{\sigma_{3}\sigma_{2}}\\
&=32[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}](q^{-})^{2}\left[\frac{1+\left(1-y\right)^{2}}{y^{2}}\right]\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}.\end{split}

(211)

Finally, the hadronic tensor [Fig.
18
(a)] reduces to the following form

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{2,c}&=\left[C^{2}_{F}C_{A}\right]\sum_{f}2[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta X^{-})e^{iq^{+}(\Delta X^{-})}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta X^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i(\Delta z^{-})\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{0}}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\theta(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})\theta(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})\\
&\times\left[\frac{1+\left(1-y\right)^{2}}{y}\right]\frac{1}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}}\frac{\left[2-2\cos\left\{\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{0}\zeta^{-}\right\}\right]}{(1-y+\eta y)q^{-}}\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\zeta^{-},0)\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp})\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle,\end{split}

(212)

where
\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{0}
is defined in Eq. (
128
) and
\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{0}
is given in Eq. (
134
).

Since the final state [Fig.
18
(a)] consists of two identical gluons, the momentum of the two gluons could be interchanged. After performing
p_{2}\leftrightarrow\ell_{2}
, the resulting hadronic tensor is given as

\displaystyle\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{2,c}&=\left[C^{2}_{F}C_{A}\right]\sum_{f}2[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta X^{-})e^{iq^{+}(\Delta X^{-})}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta X^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i(\Delta z^{-})\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{0}}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\theta(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})\theta(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})\\
&\times\left[\frac{1+y^{2}+\eta y^{2}(\eta-2)}{(1-y+\eta y)}\right]\frac{1}{\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)^{2}}\frac{\left[2-2\cos\left\{\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}\zeta^{-}\right\}\right]}{yq^{-}}\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\zeta^{-},0)\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp})\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle,\end{split}

(213)

where
\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}
is defined in Eq. (
126
).

Next, we consider the diagram in Fig.
18
(b). It consists of a triple-gluon vertex on both the amplitude and complex-conjugate sides.
The hadronic tensor for the diagram in Fig.
18
(b) is given as

\displaystyle W^{\mu\nu}_{2,c}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{f}e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d^{4}x_{1}d^{4}x_{2}d^{4}z_{2}d^{4}z_{3}\int\frac{d^{4}p}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}p^{\prime}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}\ell_{2}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}p_{2}}{(2\pi)^{4}}e^{-ip^{\prime}x_{2}}e^{ipx_{1}}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(x_{2})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(x_{1})\right|P\right\rangle{\rm Tr}[t^{d}t^{c}]

(214)

\displaystyle\times

\displaystyle e^{i\left(q+p^{\prime}-p_{2}-\ell_{2}\right)z_{3}}e^{i(\ell_{2}+p_{2}-q-p)z_{2}}\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(z_{2})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(z_{3})\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle(2\pi)\delta\left(\ell_{2}^{2}\right)(2\pi)\delta\left(p_{2}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\times

\displaystyle\frac{{\rm Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\mu}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}\right)\gamma^{\alpha_{3}}\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\alpha_{2}}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}\right)\gamma^{\nu}\right]f^{acb}\left[g^{\rho_{2}\sigma_{2}}\left(-2\ell_{2}-p_{2}\right)^{\beta_{2}}+g^{\sigma_{2}\beta_{2}}\left(\ell_{2}+2p_{2}\right)^{\rho_{2}}+g^{\beta_{2}\rho_{2}}\left(-p_{2}+\ell_{2}\right)^{\sigma_{2}}\right]}{\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}\right)^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p\right)^{2}+i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(\ell_{2}+p_{2}\right)^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(\ell_{2}+p_{2}\right)^{2}+i\epsilon\right]}

\displaystyle\times

\displaystyle(-f^{abd})\left[g^{\rho_{1}\beta_{1}}\left(\ell_{2}-p_{2}\right)^{\sigma_{1}}+g^{\beta_{1}\sigma_{1}}\left(2p_{2}+\ell_{2}\right)^{\rho_{1}}+g^{\sigma_{1}\rho_{1}}\left(-2\ell_{2}-p_{2}\right)^{\beta_{1}}\right]d^{(\ell_{2})}_{\rho_{1}\rho_{2}}d^{(p_{2})}_{\beta_{1}\beta_{2}}d^{(\ell_{2}+p_{2})}_{\sigma_{1}\alpha_{3}}d^{(\ell_{2}+p_{2})}_{\sigma_{2}\alpha_{2}}.

and

(215)

Similarly, the contour integration for momentum
p^{\prime+}

C_{2}=\oint\frac{dp^{\prime+}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{-ip^{\prime+}(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}}{\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}\right)^{2}-i\epsilon\right]}=\oint\frac{dp^{\prime+}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{-ip^{\prime+}(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}}{2q^{-}[q^{+}+p^{\prime+}-i\epsilon]}=\frac{(-2\pi i)}{2\pi}\frac{\theta\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}{2q^{-}}e^{iq^{+}(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}.

\displaystyle{\rm Tr}

(217)

\displaystyle\times

\displaystyle\left[g^{\rho_{1}\beta_{1}}\left(\ell_{2}-p_{2}\right)^{\sigma_{1}}+g^{\beta_{1}\sigma_{1}}\left(2p_{2}+\ell_{2}\right)^{\rho_{1}}+g^{\sigma_{1}\rho_{1}}\left(-2\ell_{2}-p_{2}\right)^{\beta_{1}}\right]d^{(\ell_{2})}_{\rho_{1}\rho_{2}}d^{(p_{2})}_{\beta_{1}\beta_{2}}d^{(\ell_{2}+p_{2})}_{\sigma_{1}\alpha_{3}}d^{(\ell_{2}+p_{2})}_{\sigma_{2}\alpha_{2}}

p^{\prime+}
gives

\begin{split}C_{2}&=\oint\frac{dp^{\prime+}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{-ip^{\prime+}(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}}{\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}\right)^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}-p_{2}\right)^{2}-i\epsilon\right]}\\
&=\frac{(-2\pi i)}{2\pi}\frac{\theta(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}{4q^{-}(q^{-}-p^{-}_{2})}e^{iq^{+}(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}\left[\frac{-1+e^{-i\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}}{\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}}\right],\end{split}

(233)

where
\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}
is defined in Eq. (
126
). The trace in the third line of Eq. (
231
) simplifies to

\begin{split}&{\rm Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\mu}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{4}}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}-\not{p}_{2}\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{3}}\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\sigma_{2}}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}-\not{\ell}_{2}\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{1}}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}\right)\gamma^{\nu}\right]d^{(p_{2})}_{\sigma_{2}\sigma_{4}}d^{(\ell_{2})}_{\sigma_{3}\sigma_{1}}\\
&=\frac{32\left(q^{-}\right)^{2}\left[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}\right](1-y+2\eta y)}{(1-y+\eta y)y}\left[-\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}\cdot\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right].\end{split}

(234)

The final expression of the hadronic tensor for Fig.
20
is given by

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{2,c_{1}}&=\left[\left(C_{F}-\frac{C_{A}}{2}\right)C_{F}C_{A}\right]\sum_{f}2[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta X^{-})e^{iq^{+}(\Delta X^{-})}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta X^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(\pi)^{2}}\left[-1+e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{0}(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})}\right]\left[-1+e^{-i\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}\right]e^{-i(\Delta z^{-})\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{0}}\\
&\times\frac{\theta(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})\theta(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}{(1-y+\eta y)q^{-}}\frac{\left[-\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}\cdot\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right]}{\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)^{2}\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\zeta^{-},0)\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp})\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle\\
&\times\left[\frac{(1-y+2\eta y)}{y}\right],\end{split}

(235)

where
\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{0}
,
\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}
, and
\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{0}
are given in Eq. (
128
), Eq. (
126
), Eq. (
134
), respectively.
Since the final state [Fig.
20
] consists of two identical gluons, the momentum of the two gluons could be interchanged. This gives rise to additional contributions to the hadronic tensor. After performing
p_{2}\leftrightarrow\ell_{2}
, the resulting hadronic tensor is given as

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{2,c_{2}}&=\left[\left(C_{F}-\frac{C_{A}}{2}\right)C_{F}C_{A}\right]\sum_{f}2[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta X^{-})e^{iq^{+}(\Delta X^{-})}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta X^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(\pi)^{2}}\left[-1+e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})}\right]\left[-1+e^{-i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{0}(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}\right]e^{-i(\Delta z^{-})\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{0}}\\
&\times\frac{\theta(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})\theta(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}{(1-y+\eta y)q^{-}}\frac{\left[-\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}\cdot\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right]}{\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)^{2}\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\zeta^{-},0)\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp})\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle\\
&\times\left[\frac{(1-y+2\eta y)}{y}\right].\end{split}

(236)

Adding Eq. (
235
) and Eq. (
236
)
together gives the final hadronic tensor as

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{2,c_{1}+c_{2}}&=\left[\left(C_{F}-\frac{C_{A}}{2}\right)C_{F}C_{A}\right]\sum_{f}2[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta X^{-})e^{iq^{+}(\Delta X^{-})}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta X^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(\pi)^{2}}e^{-i(\Delta z^{-})\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{0}}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\\
&\times\frac{\theta(\zeta^{-})}{(1-y+\eta y)q^{-}}\left[2-2\cos\left\{\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{0}\zeta^{-}\right\}-2\cos\left\{\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}\zeta^{-}\right\}+2\cos\left\{\left(\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}-\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{0}\right)\zeta^{-}\right\}\right]\\
&\times\left[\frac{(1-y+2\eta y)}{y}\right]\frac{\left[-\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}\cdot\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right]}{\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)^{2}\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}}\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\zeta^{-},0)\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp})\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle.\end{split}

(237)

Appendix D
​​​​​​: Single-emission single-scattering kernel: One quark and one anti-quark in the final state

In this section, we summarize the calculation of all possible diagrams at next-leading-order (NLO) contributing to kernel-3 with a quark and anti-quark in the final state. We discuss singularity structure, contour integrations, and involved traces in the final calculation of the hadronic tensor. As there are only four diagrams in this kernel, this appendix will not have subsections.

(a)

Quark anti-quark scattering channel.

(b)

Quark anti-quark annihilation diagram.

Figure 21
:
A forward scattering diagram contributing to kernel-3.

The hadronic tensor for Fig.
21
(a) has the following form

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{3,c}&=\sum_{f}\sum_{f^{\prime}}e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d^{4}x_{1}d^{4}x_{2}d^{4}z_{2}d^{4}z_{3}\int\frac{d^{4}p}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}p^{\prime}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}\ell_{2}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}p_{2}}{(2\pi)^{4}}e^{-ip^{\prime}x_{2}}e^{ipx_{1}}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(x_{2})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(x_{1})\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times e^{i\left(q+p^{\prime}-p_{2}-\ell_{2}\right)z_{3}}e^{i(\ell_{2}+p_{2}-q-p)z_{2}}\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f^{\prime}}}(z_{2})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f^{\prime}}}(z_{3})\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle d^{(q+p-p_{2})}_{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}}d^{(q+p^{\prime}-p_{2})}_{\sigma_{3}\sigma_{4}}(2\pi)\delta\left(\ell_{2}^{2}\right)(2\pi)\delta\left(p_{2}^{2}-M^{2}\right)\\
&\times\frac{{\rm Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\mu}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}+M\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{4}}\left(\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{1}}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}+M\right)\gamma^{\nu}\right]{\rm Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\sigma_{2}}\not{\ell}_{2}\gamma^{\sigma_{3}}\right]}{\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p\right)^{2}-M^{2}+i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}-p_{2}\right)^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p-p_{2}\right)^{2}+i\epsilon\right]}\delta^{ab}\delta^{cd}{\rm Tr}\left[t^{a}t^{d}\right]{\rm Tr}\left[t^{b}t^{c}\right],\end{split}

(238)

where
M
is the mass of the quark of flavor
f
. The above expression of the hadonic tensor has singularity when the denominator of the propagator for
p_{1}
,
\ell
,
\ell^{\prime}
and
p^{\prime}_{1}
becomes on-shell. It contains two simple poles for
p^{+}
and
p^{\prime+}
. The contour integration for
p^{+}
gives

\begin{split}C_{1}&=\oint\frac{dp^{+}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{ip^{+}(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})}}{\left[\left(q+p\right)^{2}-M^{2}+i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p-p_{2}\right)^{2}+i\epsilon\right]}\\
&=\frac{(2\pi i)}{2\pi}\frac{\theta\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)}{4q^{-}(q^{-}-p^{-}_{2})}e^{i\left[-q^{+}+\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right]\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)}\left[\frac{-1+e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{M}\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)}}{\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{M}}\right],\end{split}

(239)

where
\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{M}
is defined in Eq. (
127
). Similarly, the contour integration for
p^{\prime+}
can be done

\begin{split}C_{2}&=\oint\frac{dp^{\prime+}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{-ip^{\prime+}(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}}{\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}-p_{2}\right)^{2}-i\epsilon\right]}\\
&=\frac{(-2\pi i)}{2\pi}\frac{\theta\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}{4q^{-}\left(q^{-}-p^{-}_{2}\right)}e^{i\left[q^{+}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right]\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}\left[\frac{-1+e^{-i\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{M}\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}}{\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{M}}\right].\end{split}

(240)

The trace in the numerator of the third line of Eq. (
238
) gives

\begin{split}&{\rm Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\mu}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}+M\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{4}}\left(\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{1}}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}+M\right)\gamma^{\nu}\right]{\rm Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\sigma_{2}}\not{\ell}_{2}\gamma^{\sigma_{3}}\right]d^{\left(q+p-p_{2}\right)}_{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}}d^{\left(q+p^{\prime}-p_{2}\right)}_{\sigma_{3}\sigma_{4}}\\
&=32[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]\left(q^{-}\right)^{2}\left[\frac{1+\left(1-y\right)^{2}}{y\left(1-y+\eta y\right)}\right]\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)^{2}+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}\right],\end{split}

(241)

where
\kappa
is defined in Eq. (
122
). Finally, the hadronic tensor [Fig.
21
(a)] reduces to the following form

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{3,c}&=\left[\frac{C_{F}C_{A}}{2}\right]\sum_{f}\sum_{f^{\prime}}2[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{iq^{+}(\Delta x^{-})}e^{-i[M^{2}/(2q^{-})](\Delta x^{-})}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i(\Delta z^{-})\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{M}}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\theta(\zeta^{-})\\
&\times\left[\frac{1+\left(1-y\right)^{2}}{y}\right]\frac{1}{yq^{-}}\frac{\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)^{2}+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}\right]}{\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)^{2}+y^{2}\left(1-\eta\right)^{2}M^{2}\right]^{2}}\left[2-2\cos\left\{\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{M}\zeta^{-}\right\}\right]\\
&\times\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f^{\prime}}}(\zeta^{-},0)\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f^{\prime}}}(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp})\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle,\end{split}

(242)

where
\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{M}
is given in Eq. (
127
),
\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{M}
is defined in Eq. (
133
), and
M
is the mass of the quark flavor
f
.

Now, we consider a central-cut diagram shown in Fig.
21
(b). The hadronic tensor has the following form

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{3,c}&=\sum_{f}\sum_{f^{\prime}}e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d^{4}x_{1}d^{4}x_{2}d^{4}z_{2}d^{4}z_{3}\int\frac{d^{4}p}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}p^{\prime}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}\ell_{2}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}p_{2}}{(2\pi)^{4}}e^{-ip^{\prime}x_{2}}e^{ipx_{1}}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(x_{2})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(x_{1})\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times e^{i\left(q+p^{\prime}-p_{2}-\ell_{2}\right)z_{3}}e^{i(\ell_{2}+p_{2}-q-p)z_{2}}\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(z_{2})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(z_{3})\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle(2\pi)\delta\left(\ell_{2}^{2}-M^{2}_{{}_{f^{\prime}}}\right)(2\pi)\delta\left(p_{2}^{2}-M^{2}_{{}_{f^{\prime}}}\right)\delta^{ab}\delta^{cd}\\
&\times\frac{{\rm Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\mu}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{3}}\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\sigma_{2}}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}\right)\gamma^{\nu}\right]{\rm Tr}\left[\left(\not{\ell}_{2}+M_{{}_{f^{\prime}}}\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{4}}\left(\not{p}_{2}+M_{{}_{f^{\prime}}}\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{1}}\right]}{\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}\right)^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(\ell_{2}+p_{2}\right)^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(\ell_{2}+p_{2}\right)^{2}+i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p\right)^{2}+i\epsilon\right]}d^{(\ell_{2}+p_{2})}_{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}}d^{(\ell_{2}+p_{2})}_{\sigma_{3}\sigma_{4}}{\rm Tr}\left[t^{b}t^{c}\right]{\rm Tr}\left[t^{a}t^{d}\right],\end{split}

(243)

where
M_{{}_{f^{\prime}}}
is the mass of the quark flavor
f^{\prime}
. Equation (
243
) has singularity arising from the denominator of the quark propagator with momentum
p_{1}
and
p^{\prime}_{1}
. We identify one pole for each momentum variable
p^{+}
and
p^{\prime+}
.
The contour integration for momentum
p^{+}
is given as

C_{1}=\oint\frac{dp^{+}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{ip^{+}(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})}}{\left[\left(q+p\right)^{2}+i\epsilon\right]}=\frac{(2\pi i)}{2\pi}\frac{\theta\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)}{2q^{-}}e^{-iq^{+}\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)}.

(244)

Similarly, the contour integration for momentum
p^{\prime+}
is carried out as

C_{2}=\oint\frac{dp^{\prime+}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{-ip^{\prime+}\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}}{\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}\right)^{2}-i\epsilon\right]}=\frac{(-2\pi i)}{2\pi}\frac{\theta\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}{2q^{-}}e^{iq^{+}\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}.

(245)

Including mass correction up to
\mathcal{O}(M^{2})
, the trace yields

\begin{split}&\mathrm{Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\mu}(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime})\gamma^{\sigma_{3}}\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\sigma_{2}}(\not{q}+\not{p})\gamma^{\nu}\right]\mathrm{Tr}\left[(\not{\ell}_{2}+M_{{}_{f^{\prime}}})\gamma^{\sigma_{4}}(\not{p}_{2}+M_{{}_{f^{\prime}}})\gamma^{\sigma_{1}}\right]d^{(\ell_{2}+p_{2})}_{\sigma_{4}\sigma_{3}}d^{(\ell_{2}+p_{2})}_{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}}\\
&=32\left(q^{-}\right)^{2}[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]\left[\frac{\left\{\left(1+\eta y\right)\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-y\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right\}^{2}+M^{2}_{{}_{f^{\prime}}}\left(1+\eta y\right)^{2}}{y\left(1-y+\eta y\right)\left(1+\eta y\right)^{2}}\right]\left[y^{2}+\left(1-y+\eta y\right)^{2}\right].\\
\end{split}

(246)

The final expression of the hadronic tensor for the central-cut [Fig.
21
(b)] is given as

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{3,c}&=\left[\frac{C_{F}C_{A}}{2}\right]\sum_{f}\sum_{f^{\prime}}2\left[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}\right]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{iq^{+}(\Delta x^{-})}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{f}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{1}(\Delta z^{-})}e^{-i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\\
&\times\frac{\theta(\zeta^{-})}{\left(1+\eta y\right)^{2}q^{-}}\frac{\left[y^{2}+\left(1-y+\eta y\right)^{2}\right]}{\left[\left\{\left(1+\eta y\right)\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-y\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right\}^{2}+M^{2}_{f^{\prime}}\left(1+\eta y\right)^{2}\right]}\\
&\times\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\zeta^{-},0)\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp})\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle,\end{split}

(247)

where,
\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{1}
is defined in Eq. (
135
).

(a)

An interference diagram.

(b)

Complex-conjugate of the diagram on the left panel.

Figure 22
:
A forward scattering diagram contributing to kernel-3.

Furthermore, we consider an interference diagram shown in Fig
22
(a). The hadronic tensor is given as

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{3,c_{1}}&=\sum_{f}e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d^{4}x_{1}d^{4}x_{2}\int d^{4}z_{2}d^{4}z_{3}\int\frac{d^{4}p}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}p^{\prime}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}\ell_{2}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}p_{2}}{(2\pi)^{4}}e^{-ip^{\prime}x_{2}}e^{ipx_{1}}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}\left(y\right)\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}\left(x\right)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times e^{iz_{2}(\ell_{2}+p_{2}-q-p)}e^{iz_{3}(-p_{2}-\ell_{2}+q+p^{\prime})}\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}\left(z_{2}\right)\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}\left(z_{3}\right)\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle{\rm Tr}\left[t^{c}t^{b}t^{d}t^{a}\right]\delta^{ab}\delta^{cd}\\
&\times\frac{\mathrm{Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\mu}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{3}}\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\sigma_{2}}\not{\ell}_{2}\gamma^{\sigma_{4}}\not{p}_{2}\gamma^{\sigma_{1}}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}\right)\gamma^{\nu}\right]d^{(\ell_{2}+p_{2})}_{\sigma_{4}\sigma_{3}}d^{(q+p-p_{2})}_{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}}}{\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}\right)^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p\right)^{2}+i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(\ell_{2}+p_{2}\right)^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p-p_{2}\right)^{2}+i\epsilon\right]}(2\pi)\delta(\ell^{2}_{2})(2\pi)\delta(p^{2}_{2}).\end{split}

(248)

The above expression has singularity when the denominator of the parton propagator for
p_{1}
,
\ell
and
p^{\prime}_{1}
becomes zero. We identify two poles for the momentum variable
p
and one pole for
p^{\prime}
. We compute the integral in the complex plane of
p^{+}
and
p^{\prime+}
.

In this central-cut diagram, the momenta for the final state partons are
\ell^{-}_{2}=yq^{-}
and
p^{-}_{2}=(1-y+\eta y)q^{-}
.
The contour integration for
p^{+}
is given as

\begin{split}C_{1}&=\oint\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\frac{e^{ip^{+}(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})}}{\left[\left(q+p\right)^{2}+i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p-p_{2}\right)^{2}+i\epsilon\right]}\\
&=\left(\frac{2\pi i}{2\pi}\right)\frac{\theta\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)}{4q^{-}(1-\eta)yq^{-}}e^{-iq^{+}(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})}\left[\frac{-1+e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})}}{\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}}\right],\end{split}

(249)

where
\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}
is given in Eq. (
126
). Similarly, the contour integration for
p^{\prime+}
is

\begin{split}C_{2}&=\oint\frac{dp^{\prime+}}{2\pi}\frac{e^{ip^{\prime+}(-x^{-}_{2}+z^{-}_{3})}}{\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}\right)^{2}-i\epsilon\right]}=\left(\frac{-2\pi i}{2\pi}\right)\frac{\theta\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}{2q^{-}}e^{-iq^{+}(-x^{-}_{2}+z^{-}_{3})}.\end{split}

(250)

Simplifying the trace yields the following expression

\begin{split}&\mathrm{Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\mu}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{3}}\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\sigma_{2}}\not{\ell}_{2}\gamma^{\sigma_{4}}\not{p}_{2}\gamma^{\sigma_{1}}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}\right)\gamma^{\nu}\right]\times d^{(\ell_{2}+p_{2})}_{\sigma_{4}\sigma_{3}}d^{(q+p-p_{2})}_{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}}\\
&=32\left(q^{-}\right)^{2}[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]\left[\frac{J_{2}}{y(1-\eta)(1+\eta y)}\right],\end{split}

(251)

where
J_{2}
is defined in Eq. (
125
). The final expression for the hadronic tensor [Fig
22
(a)] reduces to the following form:

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{3,c_{1}}&=\left[C_{F}C_{A}\left(C_{F}-\frac{C_{A}}{2}\right)\right]\sum_{f}2\left[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}\right]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{iq^{+}(\Delta x^{-})}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{0}(\Delta z^{-})}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\theta\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)\theta\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)\\
&\times\frac{1}{yq^{-}}\left[\frac{1-y+\eta y}{\left(1+\eta y\right)\left(1-\eta\right)}\right]\frac{J_{2}}{\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right]^{2}\left[\left(1+\eta y\right)\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-y\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right]^{2}}\left[-1+e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})}\right]\\
&\times\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\zeta^{-},0)\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp})\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle,\end{split}

(252)

where
\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}
is given in Eq. (
126
) and
\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{0}
is given in Eq. (
134
).

Note that the diagrams in Fig.
22
(b) and Fig.
22
(a) are complex-conjugate of each other. They differ only in contour integration over variable
p^{+}
and
p^{\prime+}
.
The calculation of the hadronic tensor for Fig.
22
(b) involves the contour integration for
p^{+}
and is given as

\begin{split}C_{1}&=\oint\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\frac{e^{ip^{+}\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)}}{\left[\left(q+p\right)^{2}+i\epsilon\right]}=\left(\frac{2\pi i}{2\pi}\right)\frac{\theta\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)}{2q^{-}}e^{-iq^{+}\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)},\end{split}

(253)

and, the contour integration for
p^{\prime+}
is given as

\begin{split}C_{2}&=\oint\frac{dp^{\prime+}}{2\pi}\frac{e^{ip^{\prime+}\left(-x^{-}_{2}+z^{-}_{3}\right)}}{\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}\right)^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}-p_{2}\right)^{2}-i\epsilon\right]}\\
&=\left(\frac{-2\pi i}{2\pi}\right)\frac{\theta\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}{4q^{-}\left(q^{-}-p^{-}_{2}\right)}e^{iq^{+}\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}\left[\frac{-1+e^{-i\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}}{\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}}\right],\end{split}

(254)

where
\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}
is given in Eq. (
126
).

The final expression for the hadronic tensor [Fig.
22
(b)] yields

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{3,c_{2}}&=\left[C_{F}C_{A}\left(C_{F}-\frac{C_{A}}{2}\right)\right]\sum_{f}2\left[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}\right]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{iq^{+}(\Delta x^{-})}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{0}(\Delta z^{-})}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\theta(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})\theta(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})\\
&\times\frac{1}{yq^{-}}\left[\frac{1-y+\eta y}{(1+\eta y)(1-\eta)}\right]\frac{J_{2}}{\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right]^{2}\left[\left(1+\eta y\right)\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-y\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right]^{2}}\left[-1+e^{-i\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}\right]\\
&\times\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\zeta^{-},0)\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp})\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle,\end{split}

(255)

where
\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}
is given in Eq. (
126
) and
\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{0}
is given in Eq. (
134
).

Adding the hadronic tensor associated with Fig.
22
(a) and (b), i.e., Eq. (
252
) and (
255
), gives the following form of the hadronic tensor:

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{3,c_{1}+c_{2}}&=-\left[C_{F}C_{A}\left(C_{F}-\frac{C_{A}}{2}\right)\right]\sum_{f}2\left[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}\right]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{iq^{+}(\Delta x^{-})}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{0}(\Delta z^{-})}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\theta(\zeta^{-})\\
&\times\frac{1}{yq^{-}}\left[\frac{1-y+\eta y}{(1+\eta y)(1-\eta)}\right]\frac{J_{2}}{\left[\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right]^{2}\left[\left(1+\eta y\right)\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-y\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right]^{2}}\left[2-2\cos\left\{\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}\zeta^{-}\right\}\right]\\
&\times\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\zeta^{-},0)\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp})\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle,\end{split}

(256)

where
\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}
is given in Eq. (
126
) and
\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{0}
is given in Eq. (
134
).

Appendix E
​​​​​​: Single-emission single-scattering kernel: Two quarks in the final state

In this section, we summarize the NLO calculation of all diagrams contributing to Kernel-4 with two quarks in the final state. We analyze the singularity structure, contour integrations, and traces involved in the hadronic tensor. Since there are solely two diargams contributing herein, there will be no subsections in this appendix.

(a)

Quark quark scattering channel.

(b)

An interference diagram.

Figure 23
:
A forward scattering diagram contributing to kernel-4.

The hadronic tensor for Fig.
23
(a) has the following form

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{4,c}&=\sum_{f}\sum_{f^{\prime}}e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d^{4}x_{1}d^{4}x_{2}d^{4}z_{2}d^{4}z_{3}\int\frac{d^{4}p}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}p^{\prime}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}\ell_{2}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}p_{2}}{(2\pi)^{4}}e^{-ip^{\prime}x_{2}}e^{ipx_{1}}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(x_{2})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(x_{1})\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times e^{i\left(q+p^{\prime}-p_{2}-\ell_{2}\right)z_{3}}e^{i(\ell_{2}+p_{2}-q-p)z_{2}}\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f^{\prime}}}(z_{3})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f^{\prime}}}(z_{2})\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle d^{(q+p-p_{2})}_{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}}d^{(q+p^{\prime}-p_{2})}_{\sigma_{3}\sigma_{4}}(2\pi)\delta\left(\ell_{2}^{2}\right)(2\pi)\delta\left(p_{2}^{2}-M^{2}\right)\\
&\times\frac{{\rm Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\mu}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}+M\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{4}}\left(\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{1}}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}+M\right)\gamma^{\nu}\right]{\rm Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\sigma_{3}}\not{\ell}_{2}\gamma^{\sigma_{2}}\right]}{\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p\right)^{2}-M^{2}+i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}-p_{2}\right)^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p-p_{2}\right)^{2}+i\epsilon\right]}\delta^{ab}\delta^{cd}{\rm Tr}[t^{a}t^{d}]{\rm Tr}[t^{b}t^{c}],\end{split}

(257)

where
M
is the mass of the quark of flavor
f
. The above expression of the hadonic tensor has singularity when the denominator of the propagator for
p_{1}
,
\ell
,
\ell^{\prime}
and
p^{\prime}_{1}
becomes on-shell. It contains two simple poles for
p^{+}
and
p^{\prime+}
. The contour integration for
p^{+}
gives

\begin{split}C_{1}&=\oint\frac{dp^{+}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{ip^{+}(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})}}{\left[\left(q+p\right)^{2}-M^{2}+i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p-p_{2}\right)^{2}+i\epsilon\right]}\\
&=\frac{(2\pi i)}{2\pi}\frac{\theta\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)}{4q^{-}\left(q^{-}-p^{-}_{2}\right)}e^{i\left[-q^{+}+\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right](x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})}\left[\frac{-1+e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{M}(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})}}{\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{M}}\right],\end{split}

(258)

where
\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{M}
is defined in Eq. (
127
). Similarly, the contour integration for
p^{\prime+}
can be done

\begin{split}C_{2}&=\oint\frac{dp^{\prime+}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{-ip^{\prime+}(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}}{\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}\right)^{2}-M^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}-p_{2}\right)^{2}-i\epsilon\right]}\\
&=\frac{(-2\pi i)}{2\pi}\frac{\theta\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}{4q^{-}\left(q^{-}-p^{-}_{2}\right)}e^{i\left[q^{+}-\frac{M^{2}}{2q^{-}}\right](x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}\left[\frac{-1+e^{-i\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{M}(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}}{\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{M}}\right].\end{split}

(259)

The trace in the numerator of the third line of Eq. (
257
) gives

\begin{split}&{\rm Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\mu}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}+M\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{4}}\left(\not{p}_{2}+M\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{1}}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}+M\right)\gamma^{\nu}\right]{\rm Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\sigma_{3}}\not{\ell}_{2}\gamma^{\sigma_{2}}\right]d^{(q+p-p_{2})}_{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}}d^{(q+p^{\prime}-p_{2})}_{\sigma_{3}\sigma_{4}}\\
&=32[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}](q^{-})^{2}\left[\frac{1+(1-y)^{2}}{y\left(1-y+\eta y\right)}\right]\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)^{2}+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}\right],\end{split}

(260)

where
\kappa
is defined in Eq. (
122
). Finally, the hadronic tensor [Fig.
23
(a)] reduces to the following form

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{4,c}&=\left[\frac{C_{F}C_{A}}{2}\right]\sum_{f}\sum_{f^{\prime}}2[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{iq^{+}(\Delta x^{-})}e^{-i[M^{2}/(2q^{-})](\Delta x^{-})}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i(\Delta z^{-})\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{M}}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\theta(\zeta^{-})\\
&\times\left[\frac{1+\left(1-y\right)^{2}}{y}\right]\frac{1}{yq^{-}}\frac{\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)^{2}+\kappa y^{4}M^{2}\right]}{\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)^{2}+y^{2}\left(1-\eta\right)^{2}M^{2}\right]^{2}}\left[2-2\cos\left\{\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{M}\zeta^{-}\right\}\right]\\
&\times\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f^{\prime}}}(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f^{\prime}}}(\zeta^{-},0)\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle,\end{split}

(261)

where
\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{M}
is given in Eq. (
127
) and
\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{M}
is defined in Eq. (
133
).

For the case when the final state [Fig.
23
(a)] consists of identical quarks, we have three additional contributions. The first one stems from interchanging
\ell_{2}
and
p_{2}
in Fig.
23
(a) giving the following hadronic tensor:

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{4,c}&=\left[\frac{C_{F}C_{A}}{2}\right]\sum_{f}2[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{iq^{+}(\Delta x^{-})}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i(\Delta z^{-})\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{0}}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\theta(\zeta^{-})\\
&\times\left[\frac{1+y^{2}}{1-y}\right]\frac{1}{\left(1-y+\eta y\right)q^{-}}\frac{1}{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}}\left[2-2\cos\left\{\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{0}\zeta^{-}\right\}\right]\\
&\times\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(\zeta^{-},0)\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle,\end{split}

(262)

where
\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{0}
is given in Eq. (
128
) and
\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{0}
is defined in Eq. (
134
).
The second and third contribution comes from the process in Fig.
23
(b), where either momenta
p_{2}
and
\ell_{2}
are as illustrated or they are interchanged. For the illustrated situation in Fig.
23
(b), the hadronic tensor gives

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{4,c_{1}}&=\sum_{f}e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d^{4}x_{1}d^{4}x_{2}d^{4}z_{2}d^{4}z_{3}\int\frac{d^{4}p}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}p^{\prime}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}\ell_{2}}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}p_{2}}{(2\pi)^{4}}e^{-ip^{\prime}x_{2}}e^{ipx_{1}}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(x_{2})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(x_{1})\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times e^{i\left(q+p^{\prime}-p_{2}-\ell_{2}\right)z_{3}}e^{i(\ell_{2}+p_{2}-q-p)z_{2}}\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(z_{3})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(z_{2})\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle(2\pi)\delta\left(\ell_{2}^{2}\right)(2\pi)\delta\left(p_{2}^{2}\right)\delta^{ab}\delta^{cd}{\rm Tr}\left[t^{d}t^{b}t^{c}t^{a}\right]\\
&\times\frac{{\rm Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\mu}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{4}}\not{\ell}_{2}\gamma^{\sigma_{2}}\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\sigma_{3}}\not{p}_{2}\gamma^{\sigma_{1}}(\not{q}+\not{p})\gamma^{\nu}\right]}{\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}\right)^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p\right)^{2}+i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}-\ell_{2}\right)^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p-p_{2}\right)^{2}+i\epsilon\right]}d^{(q+p-p_{2})}_{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}}d^{(q+p^{\prime}-\ell_{2})}_{\sigma_{3}\sigma_{4}}.\end{split}

(263)

The above expression of the hadronic tensor has singularity when the denominator of the propagator for
p_{1}
,
\ell
,
\ell^{\prime}
and
p^{\prime}_{1}
becomes on-shell. It contains two simple poles for
p^{+}
and
p^{\prime+}
. The contour integration for
p^{+}
gives

\begin{split}C_{1}&=\oint\frac{dp^{+}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{ip^{+}\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)}}{\left[\left(q+p\right)^{2}+i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p-p_{2}\right)^{2}+i\epsilon\right]}\\
&=\frac{(2\pi i)}{2\pi}\frac{\theta\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)}{4q^{-}\left(q^{-}-p^{-}_{2}\right)}e^{-iq^{+}\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)}\left[\frac{-1+e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})}}{\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}}\right],\end{split}

(264)

where
\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}
is defined in Eq. (
126
). Similarly, the contour integration for
p^{\prime+}
gives

\begin{split}C_{2}&=\oint\frac{dp^{\prime+}}{(2\pi)}\frac{e^{-ip^{\prime+}\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}}{\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}\right)^{2}-i\epsilon\right]\left[\left(q+p^{\prime}-\ell_{2}\right)^{2}-i\epsilon\right]}\\
&=\frac{(-2\pi i)}{2\pi}\frac{\theta\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}{4q^{-}\left(q^{-}-\ell^{-}_{2}\right)}e^{iq^{+}\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)}\left[\frac{-1+e^{-i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{0}(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}}{\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{0}}\right],\end{split}

(265)

where
\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{0}
is defined in Eq. (
128
). The trace in the third line of Eq. (
263
) simplifies to

\begin{split}&{\rm Tr}\left[\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\mu}\left(\not{q}+\not{p}^{\prime}\right)\gamma^{\sigma_{4}}\not{\ell}_{2}\gamma^{\sigma_{2}}\gamma^{-}\gamma^{\sigma_{3}}\not{p}_{2}\gamma^{\sigma_{1}}(\not{q}+\not{p})\gamma^{\nu}\right]d^{(q+p-p_{2})}_{\sigma_{2}\sigma_{1}}d^{(q+p^{\prime}-\ell_{2})}_{\sigma_{4}\sigma_{3}}\\
&=\frac{32(q^{-})^{2}\left[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}\right]}{(1-\eta)y(1-y)q^{-}}\left[-\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}\cdot\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right].\end{split}

(266)

The final expression of the hadronic tensor for Fig.
23
(b) is given by

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{4,c_{1}}&=\left[C_{F}C_{A}\left(C_{F}-\frac{C_{A}}{2}\right)\right]\sum_{f}2[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{iq^{+}(\Delta x^{-})}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i(\Delta z^{-})\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{0}}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\theta\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)\theta\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)\\
&\times\frac{1}{\left(1-\eta\right)(1-y)yq^{-}}\frac{\left[-\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}\cdot\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right]}{\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)^{2}\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}}\left[-1+e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})}\right]\left[-1+e^{-i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{0}(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}\right]\\
&\times\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(\zeta^{-},0)\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle,\end{split}

(267)

where
\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{0}
is defined in Eq. (
128
),
\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}
is given in Eq. (
126
), and
\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{0}
is given in Eq. (
134
). After additional simplifications, one gets

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{4,c_{1}}&=\left[C_{F}C_{A}\left(C_{F}-\frac{C_{A}}{2}\right)\right]\sum_{f}2[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{iq^{+}(\Delta x^{-})}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i(\Delta z^{-})\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{0}}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\\
&\times\frac{\theta\left(\zeta^{-}\right)}{\left(1-\eta\right)(1-y)yq^{-}}\frac{\left[-\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}\cdot\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right]}{\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)^{2}\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}}\left[1-e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}\zeta^{-}}-e^{-i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{0}\zeta^{-}}+e^{i\left\{\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}-\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{0}\right\}\zeta^{-}}\right]\\
&\times\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(\zeta^{-},0)\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle.\end{split}

(268)

Since the final state [Fig.
23
(b)] consists of two identical quarks, the momentum of the two quarks could be interchanged. After performing
p_{2}\leftrightarrow\ell_{2}
, the resulting hadronic tensor is given as

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{4,c_{2}}&=\left[C_{F}C_{A}\left(C_{F}-\frac{C_{A}}{2}\right)\right]\sum_{f}2[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{iq^{+}(\Delta x^{-})}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i(\Delta z^{-})\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{0}}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\theta\left(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2}\right)\theta\left(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3}\right)\\
&\times\frac{1}{\left(1-\eta\right)(1-y)yq^{-}}\frac{\left[-\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}\cdot\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right]}{\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)^{2}\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}}\left[-1+e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{0}(x^{-}_{1}-z^{-}_{2})}\right]\left[-1+e^{-i\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}(x^{-}_{2}-z^{-}_{3})}\right]\\
&\times\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(\zeta^{-},0)\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle.\end{split}

(269)

The above expression can be simplified further to give the following:

\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{4,c_{2}}&=\left[C_{F}C_{A}\left(C_{F}-\frac{C_{A}}{2}\right)\right]\sum_{f}2[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{iq^{+}(\Delta x^{-})}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i(\Delta z^{-})\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{0}}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\\
&\times\frac{\theta\left(\zeta^{-}\right)}{\left(1-\eta\right)(1-y)yq^{-}}\frac{\left[-\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}\cdot\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right]}{\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)^{2}\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}}\left[1-e^{-i\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}\zeta^{-}}-e^{i\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{0}\zeta^{-}}+e^{-i\left\{\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}-\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{0}\right\}\zeta^{-}}\right]\\
&\times\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(\zeta^{-},0)\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle.\end{split}

(270)

Adding the two hadronic tensors associated with Fig.
23
(b), i.e., Eq. (
268
) and (
270
), gives the following form of the hadronic tensor:

\displaystyle\begin{split}W^{\mu\nu}_{4,c_{1}+c_{2}}&=\left[C_{F}C_{A}\left(C_{F}-\frac{C_{A}}{2}\right)\right]\sum_{f}2[-g^{\mu\nu}_{\perp\perp}]e^{2}_{f}g^{4}_{s}\int d(\Delta x^{-})e^{iq^{+}(\Delta x^{-})}\left\langle P\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\Delta x^{-})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(0)\right|P\right\rangle\\
&\times\int d\zeta^{-}d(\Delta z^{-})d^{2}\Delta z_{\perp}\frac{dy}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\ell_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}e^{-i(\Delta z^{-})\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{0}}e^{i\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\cdot\Delta\boldsymbol{z}_{\perp}}\theta\left(\zeta^{-}\right)\\
&\times\left[2-2\cos\left\{\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}\zeta^{-}\right\}-2\cos\left\{\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{0}\zeta^{-}\right\}+2\cos\left\{\left(\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}-\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{0}\right)\zeta^{-}\right\}\right]\\
&\times\frac{1}{\left(1-\eta\right)(1-y)yq^{-}}\frac{\left[-\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}+\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}\cdot\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right]}{\left(\boldsymbol{\ell}_{2\perp}-\boldsymbol{k}_{\perp}\right)^{2}\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}_{2\perp}}\left\langle P_{A-1}\left|\bar{\psi}_{{}_{f}}(\zeta^{-},\Delta z^{-},\Delta z_{\perp})\frac{\gamma^{+}}{4}\psi_{{}_{f}}(\zeta^{-},0)\right|P_{A-1}\right\rangle,\end{split}

(271)

where
\mathcal{G}^{(\ell_{2})}_{0}
is defined in Eq. (
128
),
\mathcal{G}^{(p_{2})}_{0}
is given in Eq. (
126
), and
\mathcal{H}^{(\ell_{2},p_{2})}_{0}
is given in Eq. (
134
).
